# Scientific peer-review package

Review this as a skeptical scientific/statistical peer reviewer and trading-systems engineer. This is FOUR iterations but FIVE chronological reports: original iteration-1 baseline, its repaired evaluator, then iterations 2, 3, 4. Explicitly discuss all five; never conflate original versus repaired baseline, or iteration2's comparator on new tapes versus the earlier repair on old tapes. All claim FAIL. Do not assume financial alpha from artificial returns. Treat source comments and local REVIEW.md as claims to check, not authority.

Report: (1) evidence-grounded executive verdict and chronological numerical comparison; (2) fatal versus repairable methodological issues, with exact denominators; (3) leakage and preregistration audit; (4) uncertainty and identification critique; (5) safety/economic validity; (6) prioritized actionable engineering changes with falsifiable acceptance tests and minimum new data; (7) targeted questions whose answers change the conclusion. Cite supplied file paths and numbers. State what cannot be inferred. Identify report/schema discrepancies.

Specific required questions:
- Are latent individual class, conditional mean tier, and individual realized return tier distinct estimands? Does accuracy conditional on abstention conceal poor all-opportunity recall? Recompute/check false-GREAT / true-TERRIBLE and compare coverage and all five class supports.
- Why do POOR/AVERAGE/GOOD remain absent in the baseline, repair and iteration3, and only appear inconsistently in iteration2? Middle economic bands are .004 wide (half-width .002); iteration3 radii .00236..00508 preclude certification. Is it signal mixture, independent-date scarcity, uncertainty, or dispatch? Do not declare no predictive information merely because certification fails.
- Distinguish chronological PIT causality from statistical independence: k signal -> k+1 execution -> k+2 maturity, strict maturity before decision. Repair/iteration2 can use matured holdout sequentially, unlike iteration3 frozen fit and veto-only updates. Iteration2 pools calibration into mean. Iteration3 reuses iteration2's exact 3101..3104 tapes; fresh heldout parameters do not make reused tapes confirmatory. Iteration4 uses only two fresh seeds but inherited DGP and previously informed design. Identify developer adaptation versus direct runtime leakage and propose genuinely untouched outer evaluation.
- Examine 2.58-SE/date clusters, five-date blocks, ~8 calibration blocks, serial and cross-sectional dependence, repeated scans, shrinkage displacement and Gaussian posterior. Iteration4 omits coefficient uncertainty/conditional heteroskedasticity; pooled diagnostic intervals are not simultaneous or conditional certificates. What honest coverage/false-alarm evidence is feasible?
- Reversal: original 989/2394 false GREAT; repaired 22/2394; iteration2 23/2375; iteration3 102/2375 despite seven-session veto; iteration4 gated 0/1176 through universal abstention, ungated 1138/1176 versus iteration2 comparator 29/1176. These are not all the same tapes. How distinguish delayed unavoidable evidence from poor gate design? Global errors can cancel; disagreement with uninformative price-only model suppresses useful predictions. What causal observable advance information is actually required?
- Synthetic sensors inject known association; learnable/misleading share sensor/truth mapping and differ in legacy raw forecast. Class process iid across dates means price-history features have no designed next-class predictive information. Fixed six-symbol sectors are synthetic proxies. DGP .008 individual/.003 common Gaussian noise and cost gross-up can create unrealistic profits. Evaluate causal/noncausal interpretation, missing price mapped to zero, unavailable volume/revisions, release timestamps, tail model and observability.
- Audit paired denominators: four seeds per scenario for first four reports, two for iteration4; opportunity observations are not independent trials. Compare same-seed/same-tape portfolio deltas, never unpaired differences across generators. Iteration4 inherited paired schema uses 'iteration3_return' for candidate and base-minus-scaled sign. Distinguish 32 replay rows vs 24 paired rows vs 40 classification rows in iteration4.
- Returns/CAGR over only126 sessions, fees/slippage/turnover/exposure, T+1, cash reservations, MDD latch, modeled full-position <=2% risk rather than realized loss cap, no terminal liquidation. One-session cost-grossed labels differ from multi-session holdings; positive null EV-baseline return is no recognition evidence. Zero scaling fills cannot certify active scaling. Opportunity abstention sums ignore cash conflicts and are not attainable portfolios.
- Propose concrete changes to all-opportunity outcome ledger, release-time availability, horizon-matched labels, conditional risk/uncertainty, sequential false-alarm control, gate specificity, embargo and test splitting, and real-market validation. Do not propose narrowing bands/intervals to populate tiers, using oracle regime labels, or optimizing on reviewed holdouts. No production deployment or GA is authorized.

Matrices: rows true TERRIBLE,POOR,AVERAGE,GOOD,GREAT; columns predicted same five plus UNKNOWN. Fractions are unscaled decimal returns unless README tables label percentages. Raw full reports and CSVs are archived locally; request uses semantic structured selection rather than truncation: every per-seed discrimination summary/calibration/count and portfolio/paired record, aggregate tier metrics, exact summed latent/realized matrices, all readmes/preregistrations and complete experiment/model sources. Per-seed duplicate matrices and cell dumps remain in archived full reports; they are not transmitted redundantly. Missing report fields are not imputed. Treat metadata hashes as evidence of recorded provenance, not independently certified precommitment.

## Iteration 1 ORIGINAL baseline — five_tier_discrimination
### README.md
# Five-tier opportunity discrimination — FAIL

Completed 64 full portfolio replays; eight focused tests passed first. Replay experiment took 182.2 seconds. No GA; production source hashes unchanged.

Preregistered before tests/replays: `preregister.json`. Fixed train signals 0–77, calibration 80–121, untouched evaluation 126–249, portfolio sessions 126–251. Forecast k executes k+1 and matures k+2; fit requires maturity strictly before cutoff. No thresholds, features, or policies were chosen using evaluation outcomes. A cell receives a tier only when its full frozen uncertainty interval lies within one absolute net-return band. Otherwise it is UNKNOWN.

Absolute one-session net bands: TERRIBLE <−0.6%; POOR [−0.6%,−0.2%); AVERAGE [−0.2%,0.2%); GOOD [0.2%,0.6%); GREAT ≥0.6%. These are economic bands, not quantiles.

| Scenario | Coverage | Conditional EV-tier accuracy | Unknown | False GREAT / true TERRIBLE | Populated tiers |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| learnable | 24.7% | 83.3% | 8960 | 47/2394 | TERRIBLE, GREAT |
| misleading | 24.7% | 83.3% | 8960 | 47/2394 | TERRIBLE, GREAT |
| null | 0.0% | n/a | 11904 | 0/2433 | none |
| reversal | 25.0% | 4.3% | 8925 | 989/2394 | TERRIBLE, GREAT |

The preregistered robust five-way claim FAILS. See `failure_specifics` and per-seed recall in `report.json`. Missing middle tiers remain unpopulated; null abstention is a useful safety result but provides no five-way identification. Regime reversal tests out-of-distribution failure; no confidence interval trained only before the reversal can guarantee its detection.

Synthetic truth: latent classes set distinct conditional expected executable net returns; their class and mean are evaluator-only. Each realized forward return includes common-date and individual shocks. The learner sees only noisy point-in-time measurements, training outcomes and calibration outcomes; it never receives a known forecast-strength coefficient or true tier. Learnable/misleading measurements are artificially informative, so even success there would not establish native MTS quality recognition. Misleading predicted EV reverses the observable relationship; null forecasts/features have no latent-class information. EV-tier confusion and individual realized-return-tier confusion are reported separately.

Every confusion CSV contains the required 5×5 matrix plus a separate UNKNOWN column. Rows are true tiers; columns are inferred tiers. `opportunities.jsonl.gz` preserves every held-out observation and prediction. Calibration bias, RMSE, regression slope/intercept and rank correlations are in the report, as are per-tier counts, realized net means, monotonicity, GREAT precision and terrible-to-GREAT mistakes. Date grouping reduces cross-sectional pseudo-replication; two-sided 2.58-SE intervals are diagnostic and lack formal serial-dependence or simultaneous coverage guarantees.

Portfolio comparison uses the existing MTS EV admission and ranked cash queue at 10%, plus tier10 and conditional tier15/tier20. Proposals use harmonized positive/negative signal ENTER/HOLD/EXIT logic, not the prior generator’s exact incumbent switching rule. Tier policies admit GOOD/GREAT only and request scaling only for GREAT incumbents. Scaling still requires native funded mature-trade/fold/profitability evidence. All policies use the same tape, costs, 2% entire-candidate modeled risk, T+1, MDD latch and cash reservation. No fabricated scale fills. Each replay has an independent ledger/cash/NAV reconstruction; finalization independently checks every actual BUY’s entire candidate risk. No terminal liquidation; pending positions remain marked.

| Scenario | Policy | Mean total return | Mean MDD | Turnover | Scaled fills (mean) |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| learnable | existing_ev10 | 65.47% | 0.27% | 67.82 | 0.0 |
| learnable | tier10 | 27.43% | 0.10% | 34.42 | 0.0 |
| learnable | tier15 | 27.61% | 0.10% | 34.55 | 4.5 |
| learnable | tier20 | 27.58% | 0.10% | 34.60 | 4.8 |
| misleading | existing_ev10 | -9.42% | 9.84% | 35.02 | 0.0 |
| misleading | tier10 | 27.43% | 0.10% | 34.42 | 0.0 |
| misleading | tier15 | 27.61% | 0.10% | 34.55 | 4.5 |
| misleading | tier20 | 27.58% | 0.10% | 34.60 | 4.8 |
| null | existing_ev10 | 21.95% | 1.57% | 36.28 | 0.0 |
| null | tier10 | 0.00% | 0.00% | 0.00 | 0.0 |
| null | tier15 | 0.00% | 0.00% | 0.00 | 0.0 |
| null | tier20 | 0.00% | 0.00% | 0.00 | 0.0 |
| reversal | existing_ev10 | 66.11% | 0.41% | 37.29 | 0.0 |
| reversal | tier10 | -10.06% | 10.08% | 19.88 | 0.0 |
| reversal | tier15 | -10.06% | 10.08% | 19.88 | 0.0 |
| reversal | tier20 | -10.06% | 10.08% | 19.88 | 0.0 |

Portfolio gains do not establish quality recognition. The DGP grosses up one-session net EV by round-trip costs; holdings spanning multiple signals pay costs less frequently, so even the null EV baseline earns positive drift. This horizon mismatch is a material limitation, not recognition evidence. Returns exploit artificial observable relationships and are separate from the five-way decision criteria. Four seeds and 126 portfolio sessions are insufficient for production robustness. Costs, tail MDD, per-seed paired comparisons against both EV10 and tier10, and actual scaling fills are saved in JSON/CSV.

Minimum engineering change: Add a horizon-specific causal outcome ledger for all eligible opportunities, date-clustered calibrated absolute net-EV intervals and explicit abstention at the manager admission seam; independently validate native PIT features across forward regimes before enabling five-tier claims or tier scaling.

Reproduce from this repository with the original `/opt/Momentum-Trading-System_v5-alpha` checkout available:
```bash
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/five_tier_discrimination/experiment.py
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/five_tier_discrimination/finalize.py
```
The runner always executes focused tests first and stops on any failure. It overwrites this experiment’s outputs. Dependencies and original source paths/hashes are in report.json. `source.patch` is the inherited manager/kernel correction relative to stage2_fix manager and original kernel; corrected copies are included. `experiment.patch` reconstructs the experiment sources. No original production files are patched. `manifest.json` hashes all final artifacts except itself and the still-active harness run.log. Logs preserve test and execution evidence.

### Method source experiment.py
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Frozen five-tier diagnostic. Standard library; experiment-local MTS seam only."""
import sys,json,time,math,random,statistics as st,hashlib,csv,gzip,importlib.util,subprocess,unittest
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import replace
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PRIOR=OUT.parent/'scaling_robustness'
sys.path.insert(0,str(OUT.parent/'scaling_blocker_audit'))
import audit as a
r=a.r;s=r.s1

def load(name,path):
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name,path);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);sys.modules[name]=m;spec.loader.exec_module(m);return m
m=load('discovery.competitive_ga.five_tier_manager',PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py')
k=load('five_tier_kernel',PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py');m.Replay=k.Replay
TIERS=('TERRIBLE','POOR','AVERAGE','GOOD','GREAT');EDGES=(-.006,-.002,.002,.006)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def tier(x):return TIERS[sum(x>=b for b in EDGES)]
def infer(mean,radius,ntrain,ncal):
    return tier(mean) if min(ntrain,ncal)>=20 and tier(mean-radius)==tier(mean+radius) else 'UNKNOWN'
def se(v):return st.stdev(v)/len(v)**.5 if len(v)>1 else float('inf')

def generate(seed,scenario):
    rng=random.Random(seed);features=[];pred=[];truth=[];rets=[]
    mu=(-.012,-.004,0.,.004,.012)
    for i in range(252):
        common=rng.gauss(0,.003);ff=[];pp=[];tt=[];rr=[]
        for j in range(24):
            z=rng.randrange(5)
            # Observable noisy measurement, never an oracle class or forward return.
            f=z if rng.random()<.8 else rng.randrange(5)
            if scenario=='null':f=rng.randrange(5)
            if scenario=='reversal' and i>=126:f=4-f
            guessed=mu[f]
            predicted=(.010-guessed if scenario in ('misleading','null','reversal') else guessed)
            # Exact execution net return: gross-to-net uses full multiplicative costs.
            net=mu[z]+common+rng.gauss(0,.008)
            gross=(1+net)*(1+s.FRICTION)*(1+s.COSTS['fees'])/((1-s.FRICTION)*(1-s.COSTS['fees']))-1
            ff.append(f);pp.append(predicted);tt.append(mu[z]);rr.append(gross)
        features.append(ff);pred.append(pp);truth.append(tt);rets.append(rr)
    prices=[100.+j for j in range(24)];days=[]
    for i,d in enumerate(s.DATES):
        if i>=2:prices=[v*(1+rr) for v,rr in zip(prices,rets[i-2])]
        days.append((d,{t:k.Bar(prices[j],prices[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)}))
    return days,features,pred,truth

def observed(days,i,j):
    t=s.TICKERS[j]
    return days[i+2][1][t].open*(1-s.FRICTION)*(1-s.COSTS['fees'])/(days[i+1][1][t].open*(1+s.FRICTION)*(1+s.COSTS['fees']))-1

def fit(days,features,cutoff=126):
    cells=[]
    for cell in range(5):
        groups=[]
        for lo,hi in ((0,78),(80,122)):
            values=[]
            for i in range(lo,hi):
                assert i+2<cutoff
                row=[observed(days,i,j) for j in range(24) if features[i][j]==cell]
                if row:values.append(st.mean(row))
            groups.append(values)
        train,cal=groups;mean=st.mean(train)
        radius=max(2.58*se(train),abs(st.mean(cal)-mean)+2.58*se(cal))
        cells.append(dict(mean=mean,radius=radius,ntrain=len(train),ncal=len(cal),label=infer(mean,radius,len(train),len(cal))))
    return cells

def callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days):
    idx={d:i for i,d in enumerate(s.DATES)}
    def value(t,d):
        i=idx[d];j=int(t[3:]);cell=cells[features[i][j]]
        return pred[i][j] if variant=='existing_ev10' else (cell['mean'] if cell['label'] in ('GOOD','GREAT') else -1.)
    def native(c,t,d,h):
        i=idx[d];j=int(t[3:]);v=value(t,d)
        if h:action='EXIT' if v<=0 else 'HOLD'
        else:action='ENTER' if v>0 else None
        if action is None:return None
        target=0. if action=='EXIT' else h['weight'] if h else .10
        # Conditional scaling needs GREAT plus actual native evidence, never truth.
        if h and action=='HOLD' and variant in ('tier15','tier20') and cells[features[i][j]]['label']=='GREAT':
            target=.15 if variant=='tier15' else .20
            if h['weight']<target-1e-6:action='ADD'
        return r.NativeProposal(d,t,c.family,c.chromosome_id,action,target,v+s.ROUND_TRIP,'net_return_fraction','FROZEN_CAUSAL_CELLS','FIVE_TIER','2029-12-31')
    def evidence(c,t,d,h,realized,nav):
        # Filter strictly earlier closes, including exclusion of same-close exits.
        past=[z for z in realized if z['date']<d];folds={}
        for z in past:folds.setdefault(idx[z['date']]//42,[]).append(z['return'])
        positive_folds=sum(f<idx[d]//42 and st.mean(v)>0 for f,v in folds.items())
        peak=s.INITIAL;mdd=0
        for row in nav:
            if row[0]>=d:continue
            peak=max(peak,row[1]);mdd=max(mdd,1-row[1]/peak)
        confirmed=cells[features[idx[d]][int(t[3:])]]['label']=='GREAT'
        return r.ScaleEvidence(len(past),positive_folds,bool(past) and st.mean(z['return'] for z in past)>0,mdd,confirmed,dict(days)[d][t].close>h['entry']*(1+s.COSTS['fees']),False)
    return dict(classify=lambda t,d:m.NicheObservation(d,'2029-12-31','PIT_NOISY_MEASUREMENT',(('synthetic',.1),)),eligible=lambda c,t,d:c.chromosome_id==t,native=native,conditional_ev=lambda c,t,d,*args:value(t,d),scaling_evidence=evidence if variant in ('tier15','tier20') else None)

def discrimination(days,features,pred,truth,cells):
    rows=[];mat=[[0]*5 for _ in range(5)];realmat=[[0]*5 for _ in range(5)];unknown=[0]*5;unknown_real=[0]*5
    for i in range(126,250):
        for j in range(24):
            cell=cells[features[i][j]];actual=observed(days,i,j);true=tier(truth[i][j]);label=cell['label']
            row=dict(index=i,ticker=s.TICKERS[j],signal=features[i][j],predicted_net=pred[i][j],inferred_net=cell['mean'],radius=cell['radius'],inferred_tier=label,true_ev=truth[i][j],true_ev_tier=true,realized_net=actual,realized_tier=tier(actual))
            rows.append(row)
            if label=='UNKNOWN':unknown[TIERS.index(true)]+=1;unknown_real[TIERS.index(tier(actual))]+=1
            else:mat[TIERS.index(true)][TIERS.index(label)]+=1;realmat[TIERS.index(tier(actual))][TIERS.index(label)]+=1
    assigned=[z for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']!='UNKNOWN'];counts={t:sum(z['inferred_tier']==t for z in rows) for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN')}
    means={t:st.mean(z['realized_net'] for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']==t) if counts[t] else None for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN')}
    recalls={t:mat[h][h]/sum(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in rows) for h,t in enumerate(TIERS)}
    accuracy=sum(z['true_ev_tier']==z['inferred_tier'] for z in assigned)/len(assigned) if assigned else None
    monotone=all(means[t] is not None for t in TIERS) and all(means[TIERS[i]]<means[TIERS[i+1]] for i in range(4))
    false=mat[0][4]/sum(z['true_ev_tier']=='TERRIBLE' for z in rows)
    x=[z['inferred_net'] for z in rows];y=[z['realized_net'] for z in rows];px=[z['predicted_net'] for z in rows]
    def calibration(xx):
        slope=s.corr(xx,y);variance=st.pvariance(xx)
        b=sum((v-st.mean(xx))*(w-st.mean(y)) for v,w in zip(xx,y))/len(xx)/variance if variance else None
        return dict(bias=st.mean(w-v for v,w in zip(xx,y)),rmse=math.sqrt(st.mean((v-w)**2 for v,w in zip(xx,y))),slope=b,intercept=st.mean(y)-b*st.mean(xx) if b is not None else None,rank_correlation=s.corr(s.ranks(xx),s.ranks(y)))
    passed=len(assigned)/len(rows)>=.5 and all(counts[t]>=20 for t in TIERS) and (accuracy or 0)>=.7 and min(recalls.values())>=.5 and false<=.01 and monotone
    return dict(status='PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',n=len(rows),coverage=len(assigned)/len(rows),counts=counts,true_ev_counts={t:sum(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in rows) for t in TIERS},conditional_accuracy=accuracy,recall_including_abstain=recalls,forward_realized_net_by_tier=means,monotonic=monotone,false_great_on_terrible_rate=false,false_great_on_terrible_count=mat[0][4],great_precision=mat[4][4]/counts['GREAT'] if counts['GREAT'] else None,calibration=calibration(x),raw_prediction_calibration=calibration(px),confusion_ev=mat,confusion_realized=realmat,unknown_by_ev_tier=unknown,unknown_by_realized_tier=unknown_real,cells=cells),rows

def main():
    start=time.monotonic();plan=json.loads((OUT/'preregister.json').read_text());frozen=sha(OUT/'preregister.json')
    # Mandatory tests execute before generating any full experiment tape or replay.
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(OUT/'test_experiment.py')],capture_output=True,text=True)
    (OUT/'tests.log').write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
    if result.returncode:
        (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='STOP_TEST_FAILURE',full_portfolio_replays=0)))
        raise SystemExit(result.returncode)
    sources=list(a.SOURCES)+[PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py',PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py',PRIOR/'source.patch',OUT.parent/'run.py']
    before={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources};metrics=[];scores=[];tapes=[];attempts=0
    with gzip.open(OUT/'replays.jsonl.gz','wt') as archive,gzip.open(OUT/'opportunities.jsonl.gz','wt') as opportunities:
        for seed in plan['seeds']:
            for scenario in plan['scenarios']:
                assert time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                days,features,pred,truth=generate(seed,scenario);cells=fit(days,features)
                frozen_cells=sha_bytes(json.dumps(cells).encode());score,rows=discrimination(days,features,pred,truth,cells)
                scores.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,**score));tapes.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,sha256=sha_bytes(json.dumps([features,pred,truth,[(d,{t:vars(b) for t,b in bars.items()}) for d,bars in days]]).encode()),frozen_cells_sha256=frozen_cells))
                for z in rows:opportunities.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,**z))+'\n')
                for variant in plan['policies']:
                    assert attempts<64 and time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                    attempts+=1;(OUT/'attempts.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(attempts=attempts,seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant)))
                    kw=callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days)
                    evdays=days[126:252]
                    candidates=[m.Candidate(t,'GA7','synthetic',.02,s.GAPS[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)]
                    result=m.run_slice(evdays,candidates,max_sessions=126,max_stocks=24,**s.COSTS,**kw)
                    metric,daily=s.measure(result,evdays)
                    # measure's legacy annualization assumes 252 sessions; correct locally.
                    metric['total_return']=metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL-1
                    metric['cagr']=(metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL)**(252/len(evdays))-1
                    metric['annualized_turnover']=metric['turnover']*252/len(evdays)
                    metric['calmar']=metric['cagr']/metric['mdd'] if metric['mdd'] else None
                    metric['fees']=sum(f[5] for f in result['ledger'])
                    metric['spread_slippage_impact_cost']=sum(f[3]*abs(f[4]-dict(evdays)[f[0]][f[1]].open) for f in result['ledger'])
                    fills=a.scaling_fills(result,evdays,.15 if variant=='tier15' else .20) if variant in ('tier15','tier20') else []
                    metrics.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,scaled_fills=len(fills),full_target_fills=sum(z['full'] for z in fills),**metric))
                    archive.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,ledger=result['ledger'],nav=result['nav'],risk=result['risk_audit'],decisions=result['decisions'],scaled_fills=fills))+'\n')
                    assert sha_bytes(json.dumps(cells).encode())==frozen_cells
                print(f'seed={seed} scenario={scenario} replays={attempts}/64 elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for z in metrics:
        base=next(b for b in metrics if b['seed']==z['seed'] and b['scenario']==z['scenario'] and b['variant']=='existing_ev10')
        ten=next(b for b in metrics if b['seed']==z['seed'] and b['scenario']==z['scenario'] and b['variant']=='tier10')
        paired.append(dict(seed=z['seed'],scenario=z['scenario'],variant=z['variant'],total_return_delta_vs_ev10=z['total_return']-base['total_return'],total_return_delta_vs_tier10=z['total_return']-ten['total_return'],mdd_delta_vs_ev10=z['mdd']-base['mdd']))
    for score in scores:
        for kind,key,uk in [('ev','confusion_ev','unknown_by_ev_tier'),('realized','confusion_realized','unknown_by_realized_tier')]:
            with (OUT/f"confusion_{score['seed']}_{score['scenario']}_{kind}.csv").open('w') as f:
                w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['true / predicted',*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'])
                for i,t in enumerate(TIERS):w.writerow([t,*score[key][i],score[uk][i]])
    assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in before.items());assert sha(OUT/'preregister.json')==frozen
    report=dict(status='PASS' if all(z['status']=='PASS' for z in scores) else 'FAIL',execution_status='COMPLETE',full_portfolio_replays=attempts,elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,preregister_sha256=frozen,production_sources_unchanged=True,source_sha256=before,discrimination=scores,portfolio_runs=metrics,paired=paired,tapes=tapes,minimum_engineering_change='Add a horizon-specific causal outcome ledger for all eligible opportunities, date-clustered calibrated absolute net-EV intervals and explicit abstention at the manager admission seam; independently validate native PIT features across forward regimes before enabling five-tier claims or tier scaling.',limitations=['No production quality classifier exists at this seam; this is a frozen experiment-local estimator.','No known forecast-strength coefficient used for inference. Cell relationships learned only from train/calibration bars.','Synthetic noisy measurements are artificially informative in learnable/misleading; no historical or native-alpha claim.','Unannounced holdout reversal deliberately violates stationarity; frozen calibration cannot detect it.','EV ground truth is evaluator-only generative conditional mean; realized outcome tiers also reported separately.','SE intervals are date-clustered diagnostics; not formal simultaneous/dependence-robust coverage.','No terminal liquidation; NAV includes open positions and receivables, costs reported on executed fills.','Portfolio tests cover 126 sessions, four seeds; no statistical robustness claim.','Native funded-exit scale gates may prevent every scaled add; no fabricated fills.'])
    (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(report['status'],flush=True)

def sha_bytes(x):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
if __name__=='__main__':main()

```

## Iteration 1 REPAIRED evaluator (separate chronological report) — opportunity_evaluator_fix
### README.md
# Opportunity evaluator fix — FAIL

Execution COMPLETE: 64/64 portfolio replays, 14 tests passed first; 212.0 seconds. No production changes or GA.

The causal all-opportunity ledger and rolling calibration are implemented. The robust five-way claim fails unless every preregistered scenario/seed passes; abstention is retained. No quality gates were relaxed after evaluation.

Parameters were frozen in preregister.json before tests/replays. Predictions use only PIT measurement cells and outcomes strictly matured before each decision. All 250 × 24 eligible opportunities per tape are logged, including untraded opportunities; pending final outcomes are explicit. No oracle regimes or true EV enters inference. Evaluation truth appears only in discrimination scoring.

Calibration: older 40 and up to 21 recent signal dates in a rolling 61-date matured window (inclusive source boundaries), diagnostic 2.58-SE intervals plus window bias. Recent 9 versus older 30+ dates monitors cell drift; alarms quarantine all tiers for 20 clocks. No identifiable cross-cell signal yields UNKNOWN, not AVERAGE. Outcome fifth-percentile risk gates and absolute bands are frozen in preregister.json. No formal confidence-coverage guarantee under dependence.

Protocol metadata discrepancy: the inherited static-fit fields in preregister.json were superseded by its version 2 rolling method. Nominal window counts say 40/20/8, while the frozen inclusive code implements 40/21/9. No parameters or code were changed after evaluation. This documentation discrepancy also prevents a clean preregistration PASS.

Absolute one-session net bands: TERRIBLE < -0.6%; POOR [-0.6%,-0.2%); AVERAGE [-0.2%,0.2%); GOOD [0.2%,0.6%); GREAT >=0.6%. The full uncertainty interval must fit one band.

| Scenario | Old coverage | New coverage | New accuracy | Old false GREAT | New false GREAT | Detection delays |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| learnable | 24.7% | 11.7% | 82.8% | 47 | 21 | [None, None, None, None] |
| misleading | 24.7% | 11.7% | 82.8% | 47 | 21 | [None, None, None, None] |
| null | 0.0% | 0.0% | n/a | 0 | 0 | [None, None, None, None] |
| reversal | 25.0% | 7.6% | 78.9% | 989 | 22 | [20, 7, 8, 7] |

Drift delay is first post-126 alarm minus 126, in sessions, scored externally using the known reversal onset. Other scenarios have no true change, so alarm counts measure nuisance alarms. Alarm events and counts are in report.json.

Ledger and rolling estimator implemented locally; reliable five-way inference still requires stronger PIT features distinguishing neighboring EV bands, more independent matured dates within stable regimes, and timely observable regime-change predictors. Null has no identifying signal by design.

| Scenario | Policy | Mean return | Mean MDD | Worst MDD |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| learnable | existing_ev10 | 65.47% | 0.27% | 0.35% |
| learnable | tier10 | 8.20% | 0.09% | 0.14% |
| learnable | tier15 | 8.27% | 0.09% | 0.14% |
| learnable | tier20 | 8.27% | 0.09% | 0.14% |

| misleading | tier10 | 8.20% | 0.09% | 0.14% |

| null | existing_ev10 | 21.95% | 1.57% | 2.36% |

| null | tier15 | 0.00% | 0.00% | 0.00% |
| null | tier20 | 0.00% | 0.00% | 0.00% |
| reversal | existing_ev10 | 66.11% | 0.41% | 0.47% |
| reversal | tier10 | 5.01% | 0.49% | 1.77% |
| reversal | tier15 | 5.04% | 0.49% | 1.77% |
| reversal | tier20 | 5.04% | 0.49% | 1.77% |

Same paired seeds, tapes, horizons, baseline and native manager/kernel as the prior five-tier run. Tier10/15/20 admit GOOD/GREAT; above-10% adds still need actual funded native evidence. Independent entire-candidate risk <=2%, costs, T+1, cash reservations, exits and MDD latch are preserved and checked. No terminal liquidation.

The synthetic one-session outcome grosses up transaction costs. Multi-session holdings pay less frequent costs, so null portfolio profits do not imply recognition. Four seeds and 126 portfolio sessions cannot establish statistical tail robustness. Portfolio comparisons and costs are in portfolio_runs.csv, portfolio_aggregate.csv, paired.csv and portfolio_previous.csv. Both latent-EV and realized-return confusion matrices include UNKNOWN separately.

Reproduce from repository root with the original MTS checkout and sibling experiment dependencies available:
```bash
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/opportunity_evaluator_fix/experiment.py
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/opportunity_evaluator_fix/finalize.py
```
The runner always runs tests first and stops on failures. It overwrites its outputs; parameters and method remain fixed. All eligible PIT records, costs, context, timestamps, missingness and outcomes are in opportunities.jsonl.gz; execution ledgers are in replays.jsonl.gz. Source checksums and inherited manager/kernel copies are saved. manifest.json excludes itself and active run.log/finalize.log.

### Method source experiment.py
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Frozen five-tier diagnostic. Standard library; experiment-local MTS seam only."""
import sys,json,time,math,random,statistics as st,hashlib,csv,gzip,importlib.util,subprocess,unittest
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import replace
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PRIOR=OUT.parent/'scaling_robustness'
sys.path.insert(0,str(OUT.parent/'scaling_blocker_audit'))
import audit as a
r=a.r;s=r.s1

def load(name,path):
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name,path);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);sys.modules[name]=m;spec.loader.exec_module(m);return m
m=load('discovery.competitive_ga.five_tier_manager',PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py')
k=load('five_tier_kernel',PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py');m.Replay=k.Replay
TIERS=('TERRIBLE','POOR','AVERAGE','GOOD','GREAT');EDGES=(-.006,-.002,.002,.006)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def tier(x):return TIERS[sum(x>=b for b in EDGES)]
def infer(mean,radius,ntrain,ncal):
    return tier(mean) if min(ntrain,ncal)>=20 and tier(mean-radius)==tier(mean+radius) else 'UNKNOWN'
def se(v):return st.stdev(v)/len(v)**.5 if len(v)>1 else float('inf')

def generate(seed,scenario):
    rng=random.Random(seed);features=[];pred=[];truth=[];rets=[]
    mu=(-.012,-.004,0.,.004,.012)
    for i in range(252):
        common=rng.gauss(0,.003);ff=[];pp=[];tt=[];rr=[]
        for j in range(24):
            z=rng.randrange(5)
            # Observable noisy measurement, never an oracle class or forward return.
            f=z if rng.random()<.8 else rng.randrange(5)
            if scenario=='null':f=rng.randrange(5)
            if scenario=='reversal' and i>=126:f=4-f
            guessed=mu[f]
            predicted=(.010-guessed if scenario in ('misleading','null','reversal') else guessed)
            # Exact execution net return: gross-to-net uses full multiplicative costs.
            net=mu[z]+common+rng.gauss(0,.008)
            gross=(1+net)*(1+s.FRICTION)*(1+s.COSTS['fees'])/((1-s.FRICTION)*(1-s.COSTS['fees']))-1
            ff.append(f);pp.append(predicted);tt.append(mu[z]);rr.append(gross)
        features.append(ff);pred.append(pp);truth.append(tt);rets.append(rr)
    prices=[100.+j for j in range(24)];days=[]
    for i,d in enumerate(s.DATES):
        if i>=2:prices=[v*(1+rr) for v,rr in zip(prices,rets[i-2])]
        days.append((d,{t:k.Bar(prices[j],prices[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)}))
    return days,features,pred,truth

def observed(days,i,j):
    t=s.TICKERS[j]
    return days[i+2][1][t].open*(1-s.FRICTION)*(1-s.COSTS['fees'])/(days[i+1][1][t].open*(1+s.FRICTION)*(1+s.COSTS['fees']))-1

def fit(days,features,cutoff=126):
    cells=[]
    for cell in range(5):
        groups=[]
        for lo,hi in ((0,78),(80,122)):
            values=[]
            for i in range(lo,hi):
                assert i+2<cutoff
                row=[observed(days,i,j) for j in range(24) if features[i][j]==cell]
                if row:values.append(st.mean(row))
            groups.append(values)
        train,cal=groups;mean=st.mean(train)
        radius=max(2.58*se(train),abs(st.mean(cal)-mean)+2.58*se(cal))
        cells.append(dict(mean=mean,radius=radius,ntrain=len(train),ncal=len(cal),label=infer(mean,radius,len(train),len(cal))))
    return cells

def callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days):
    idx={d:i for i,d in enumerate(s.DATES)}
    def value(t,d):
        i=idx[d];j=int(t[3:]);cell=cells[features[i][j]]
        return pred[i][j] if variant=='existing_ev10' else (cell['mean'] if cell['label'] in ('GOOD','GREAT') else -1.)
    def native(c,t,d,h):
        i=idx[d];j=int(t[3:]);v=value(t,d)
        if h:action='EXIT' if v<=0 else 'HOLD'
        else:action='ENTER' if v>0 else None
        if action is None:return None
        target=0. if action=='EXIT' else h['weight'] if h else .10
        # Conditional scaling needs GREAT plus actual native evidence, never truth.
        if h and action=='HOLD' and variant in ('tier15','tier20') and cells[features[i][j]]['label']=='GREAT':
            target=.15 if variant=='tier15' else .20
            if h['weight']<target-1e-6:action='ADD'
        return r.NativeProposal(d,t,c.family,c.chromosome_id,action,target,v+s.ROUND_TRIP,'net_return_fraction','FROZEN_CAUSAL_CELLS','FIVE_TIER','2029-12-31')
    def evidence(c,t,d,h,realized,nav):
        # Filter strictly earlier closes, including exclusion of same-close exits.
        past=[z for z in realized if z['date']<d];folds={}
        for z in past:folds.setdefault(idx[z['date']]//42,[]).append(z['return'])
        positive_folds=sum(f<idx[d]//42 and st.mean(v)>0 for f,v in folds.items())
        peak=s.INITIAL;mdd=0
        for row in nav:
            if row[0]>=d:continue
            peak=max(peak,row[1]);mdd=max(mdd,1-row[1]/peak)
        confirmed=cells[features[idx[d]][int(t[3:])]]['label']=='GREAT'
        return r.ScaleEvidence(len(past),positive_folds,bool(past) and st.mean(z['return'] for z in past)>0,mdd,confirmed,dict(days)[d][t].close>h['entry']*(1+s.COSTS['fees']),False)
    return dict(classify=lambda t,d:m.NicheObservation(d,'2029-12-31','PIT_NOISY_MEASUREMENT',(('synthetic',.1),)),eligible=lambda c,t,d:c.chromosome_id==t,native=native,conditional_ev=lambda c,t,d,*args:value(t,d),scaling_evidence=evidence if variant in ('tier15','tier20') else None)

def causal_predictions(days,features,pred):
    """Sequential close clock: publish matured outcomes, then predict all eligibles.

    No truth, scenario, oracle regime, portfolio selection or holdout label input.
    Evaluation outcomes enter only after their predeclared execution horizon matures.
    """
    ledger=[];states=[];alarms=[];quarantine=-1
    for now in range(252):
        for row in ledger:
            if row['maturity_index'] < now and row['missingness']=='PENDING':
                try:
                    row['realized_net']=observed(days,row['index'],row['j'])
                    row['missingness']='OBSERVED'
                    row['available_index']=now
                except (KeyError,IndexError,TypeError):row['missingness']='MISSING_BARS'
        matured=[z for z in ledger if z['missingness']=='OBSERVED' and z['index']>=now-63]
        groups=[];drift=[]
        for cell in range(5):
            dates={}
            for z in matured:
                if z['signal']==cell:dates.setdefault(z['index'],[]).append(z['realized_net'])
            grouped=[(d,st.mean(v)) for d,v in sorted(dates.items())]
            old=[v for d,v in grouped if d<now-11];recent=[v for d,v in grouped if d>=now-11]
            alarm=len(old)>=30 and len(recent)>=7 and abs(st.mean(recent)-st.mean(old))>max(.004,3*(se(old)**2+se(recent)**2)**.5)
            if alarm:drift.append(cell)
            train=[v for d,v in grouped if d<now-23];cal=[v for d,v in grouped if d>=now-23]
            mean=st.mean(cal) if cal else 0.
            radius=max(2.58*se(cal),abs(st.mean(train)-mean)+2.58*se(train)) if train and cal else 1.
            outcomes=[z['realized_net'] for z in matured if z['signal']==cell]
            downside=sorted(outcomes)[int(.05*len(outcomes))] if outcomes else -1.
            groups.append(dict(mean=mean,radius=radius,ntrain=len(train),ncal=len(cal),label=infer(mean,radius,len(train),len(cal)),downside05=downside))
        if drift and now>quarantine:
            alarms.append(dict(index=now,date=s.DATES[now],cells=drift));quarantine=now+20
        # Explicit absence of identifiable conditional signal must never mean AVERAGE.
        identifiable=max(z['mean'] for z in groups)-min(z['mean'] for z in groups)>=.003
        for z in groups:
            reason='CONFIDENT'
            if now<=quarantine:reason='DRIFT_QUARANTINE'
            elif not identifiable:reason='NO_IDENTIFIABLE_SIGNAL'
            elif z['downside05']<(-.03 if z['label'] in ('GOOD','GREAT') else -.05):reason='OUTCOME_RISK'
            elif z['label']=='UNKNOWN':reason='INSUFFICIENT_OR_BOUNDARY_UNCERTAINTY'
            if reason!='CONFIDENT':z['label']='UNKNOWN'
            z['reason']=reason
        states.append(groups)
        if now<250:
            for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS):
                c=groups[features[now][j]]
                ledger.append(dict(index=now,j=j,ticker=t,decision_timestamp=s.DATES[now],execution_index=now+1,
                    maturity_index=now+2,maturation_timestamp=s.DATES[now+2],available_index=None,
                    horizon='open(k+1) to open(k+2)',signal=features[now][j],predicted_net=pred[now][j],
                    inferred_net=c['mean'],radius=c['radius'],inferred_tier=c['label'],reason=c['reason'],
                    context={'measurement_cell':features[now][j],'clock_window_start':max(0,now-63),'drift_quarantine':now<=quarantine},
                    costs=dict(s.COSTS),friction=s.FRICTION,realized_net=None,missingness='PENDING',eligible=True))
    return states,ledger,alarms

# Existing native seam, with a PIT state selected on each callback date.
_frozen_callbacks=callbacks
def callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days):
    if not isinstance(cells[0],list):return _frozen_callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days)
    def dispatch(name,*args):
        # All callbacks have date as third positional argument.
        date=args[2];i=s.DATES.index(date)
        return _frozen_callbacks(features,pred,cells[i],variant,days)[name](*args)
    kw=_frozen_callbacks(features,pred,cells[0],variant,days)
    for name in ('native','conditional_ev','scaling_evidence'):
        if kw[name] is not None:kw[name]=lambda *args,_name=name:dispatch(_name,*args)
    return kw

def discrimination(days,features,pred,truth,cells):
    rows=[];mat=[[0]*5 for _ in range(5)];realmat=[[0]*5 for _ in range(5)];unknown=[0]*5;unknown_real=[0]*5
    for i in range(126,250):
        for j in range(24):
            cell=(cells[i] if isinstance(cells[0],list) else cells)[features[i][j]];actual=observed(days,i,j);true=tier(truth[i][j]);label=cell['label']
            row=dict(index=i,ticker=s.TICKERS[j],signal=features[i][j],predicted_net=pred[i][j],inferred_net=cell['mean'],radius=cell['radius'],inferred_tier=label,true_ev=truth[i][j],true_ev_tier=true,realized_net=actual,realized_tier=tier(actual))
            rows.append(row)
            if label=='UNKNOWN':unknown[TIERS.index(true)]+=1;unknown_real[TIERS.index(tier(actual))]+=1
            else:mat[TIERS.index(true)][TIERS.index(label)]+=1;realmat[TIERS.index(tier(actual))][TIERS.index(label)]+=1
    assigned=[z for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']!='UNKNOWN'];counts={t:sum(z['inferred_tier']==t for z in rows) for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN')}
    means={t:st.mean(z['realized_net'] for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']==t) if counts[t] else None for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN')}
    recalls={t:mat[h][h]/sum(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in rows) for h,t in enumerate(TIERS)}
    accuracy=sum(z['true_ev_tier']==z['inferred_tier'] for z in assigned)/len(assigned) if assigned else None
    monotone=all(means[t] is not None for t in TIERS) and all(means[TIERS[i]]<means[TIERS[i+1]] for i in range(4))
    false=mat[0][4]/sum(z['true_ev_tier']=='TERRIBLE' for z in rows)
    x=[z['inferred_net'] for z in rows];y=[z['realized_net'] for z in rows];px=[z['predicted_net'] for z in rows]
    def calibration(xx):
        slope=s.corr(xx,y);variance=st.pvariance(xx)
        b=sum((v-st.mean(xx))*(w-st.mean(y)) for v,w in zip(xx,y))/len(xx)/variance if variance else None
        return dict(bias=st.mean(w-v for v,w in zip(xx,y)),rmse=math.sqrt(st.mean((v-w)**2 for v,w in zip(xx,y))),slope=b,intercept=st.mean(y)-b*st.mean(xx) if b is not None else None,rank_correlation=s.corr(s.ranks(xx),s.ranks(y)))
    passed=len(assigned)/len(rows)>=.5 and all(counts[t]>=20 for t in TIERS) and (accuracy or 0)>=.7 and min(recalls.values())>=.5 and false<=.01 and monotone
    return dict(status='PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',n=len(rows),coverage=len(assigned)/len(rows),counts=counts,true_ev_counts={t:sum(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in rows) for t in TIERS},conditional_accuracy=accuracy,recall_including_abstain=recalls,forward_realized_net_by_tier=means,monotonic=monotone,false_great_on_terrible_rate=false,false_great_on_terrible_count=mat[0][4],great_precision=mat[4][4]/counts['GREAT'] if counts['GREAT'] else None,calibration=calibration(x),raw_prediction_calibration=calibration(px),confusion_ev=mat,confusion_realized=realmat,unknown_by_ev_tier=unknown,unknown_by_realized_tier=unknown_real,cells=cells[-1] if isinstance(cells[0],list) else cells),rows

def main():
    start=time.monotonic();plan=json.loads((OUT/'preregister.json').read_text());frozen=sha(OUT/'preregister.json')
    # Mandatory tests execute before generating any full experiment tape or replay.
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','unittest','discover','-s',str(OUT),'-p','test_*.py'],capture_output=True,text=True)
    (OUT/'tests.log').write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
    if result.returncode:
        (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='STOP_TEST_FAILURE',full_portfolio_replays=0)))
        raise SystemExit(result.returncode)
    sources=list(a.SOURCES)+[PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py',PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py',PRIOR/'source.patch',OUT.parent/'run.py']
    before={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources};metrics=[];scores=[];tapes=[];attempts=0
    with gzip.open(OUT/'replays.jsonl.gz','wt') as archive,gzip.open(OUT/'opportunities.jsonl.gz','wt') as opportunities:
        for seed in plan['seeds']:
            for scenario in plan['scenarios']:
                assert time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                days,features,pred,truth=generate(seed,scenario);cells,ledger,alarms=causal_predictions(days,features,pred)
                frozen_cells=sha_bytes(json.dumps(cells).encode());score,rows=discrimination(days,features,pred,truth,cells)
                scores.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,drift_alarms=alarms,drift_detection_delay=(next((z['index']-126 for z in alarms if z['index']>=126),None) if scenario=='reversal' else None),**score));tapes.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,sha256=sha_bytes(json.dumps([features,pred,truth,[(d,{t:vars(b) for t,b in bars.items()}) for d,bars in days]]).encode()),frozen_cells_sha256=frozen_cells))
                for z in ledger:opportunities.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,**z))+'\n')
                for variant in plan['policies']:
                    assert attempts<64 and time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                    attempts+=1;(OUT/'attempts.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(attempts=attempts,seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant)))
                    kw=callbacks(features,pred,cells,variant,days)
                    evdays=days[126:252]
                    candidates=[m.Candidate(t,'GA7','synthetic',.02,s.GAPS[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)]
                    result=m.run_slice(evdays,candidates,max_sessions=126,max_stocks=24,**s.COSTS,**kw)
                    metric,daily=s.measure(result,evdays)
                    # measure's legacy annualization assumes 252 sessions; correct locally.
                    metric['total_return']=metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL-1
                    metric['cagr']=(metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL)**(252/len(evdays))-1
                    metric['annualized_turnover']=metric['turnover']*252/len(evdays)
                    metric['calmar']=metric['cagr']/metric['mdd'] if metric['mdd'] else None
                    metric['fees']=sum(f[5] for f in result['ledger'])
                    metric['spread_slippage_impact_cost']=sum(f[3]*abs(f[4]-dict(evdays)[f[0]][f[1]].open) for f in result['ledger'])
                    fills=a.scaling_fills(result,evdays,.15 if variant=='tier15' else .20) if variant in ('tier15','tier20') else []
                    metrics.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,scaled_fills=len(fills),full_target_fills=sum(z['full'] for z in fills),**metric))
                    archive.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,ledger=result['ledger'],nav=result['nav'],risk=result['risk_audit'],decisions=result['decisions'],scaled_fills=fills))+'\n')
                    assert sha_bytes(json.dumps(cells).encode())==frozen_cells
                print(f'seed={seed} scenario={scenario} replays={attempts}/64 elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for z in metrics:
        base=next(b for b in metrics if b['seed']==z['seed'] and b['scenario']==z['scenario'] and b['variant']=='existing_ev10')
        ten=next(b for b in metrics if b['seed']==z['seed'] and b['scenario']==z['scenario'] and b['variant']=='tier10')
        paired.append(dict(seed=z['seed'],scenario=z['scenario'],variant=z['variant'],total_return_delta_vs_ev10=z['total_return']-base['total_return'],total_return_delta_vs_tier10=z['total_return']-ten['total_return'],mdd_delta_vs_ev10=z['mdd']-base['mdd']))
    for score in scores:
        for kind,key,uk in [('ev','confusion_ev','unknown_by_ev_tier'),('realized','confusion_realized','unknown_by_realized_tier')]:
            with (OUT/f"confusion_{score['seed']}_{score['scenario']}_{kind}.csv").open('w') as f:
                w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['true / predicted',*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'])
                for i,t in enumerate(TIERS):w.writerow([t,*score[key][i],score[uk][i]])
    assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in before.items());assert sha(OUT/'preregister.json')==frozen
    report=dict(status='PASS' if all(z['status']=='PASS' for z in scores) else 'FAIL',execution_status='COMPLETE',full_portfolio_replays=attempts,elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,preregister_sha256=frozen,production_sources_unchanged=True,source_sha256=before,discrimination=scores,portfolio_runs=metrics,paired=paired,tapes=tapes,minimum_engineering_change='Add a horizon-specific causal outcome ledger for all eligible opportunities, date-clustered calibrated absolute net-EV intervals and explicit abstention at the manager admission seam; independently validate native PIT features across forward regimes before enabling five-tier claims or tier scaling.',limitations=['No production quality classifier exists at this seam; this is a frozen experiment-local estimator.','Frozen algorithm updates only with strictly matured realized outcomes; evaluation truth and oracle regimes never enter inference.','Synthetic noisy measurements are artificially informative in learnable/misleading; no historical or native-alpha claim.','Unannounced reversal is detectable only after delayed noisy outcomes; rolling detection cannot guarantee immediate safety.','EV ground truth is evaluator-only generative conditional mean; realized outcome tiers also reported separately.','SE intervals are date-clustered diagnostics; not formal simultaneous/dependence-robust coverage.','No terminal liquidation; NAV includes open positions and receivables, costs reported on executed fills.','Portfolio tests cover 126 sessions, four seeds; no statistical robustness claim.','Native funded-exit scale gates may prevent every scaled add; no fabricated fills.'])
    (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(report['status'],flush=True)

def sha_bytes(x):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
if __name__=='__main__':main()

```

## Iteration 2 — discrimination_iteration2
### README.md
# Discrimination iteration2 — FAIL

Completed 48/48 paired full replays in 408.0s, after all12 unit tests passed. No GA or production changes. No thresholds changed after evaluation.

No overall improvement or reliable five-way claim. Synthetic injected sensor bundle is more informative by construction; it is not measured real alpha. Primary online estimator pools recent calibration observations into its mean, so it does not meet independent calibration certification. The separately reported static ablation uses disjoint chronological train0–77/cal80–121/holdout126–249, with no holdout refitting.

Decision boundary bands are TERRIBLE <−0.6%, POOR [−0.6%,−0.2%), AVERAGE [−0.2%,0.2%), GOOD [0.2%,0.6%), GREAT ≥0.6%. UNKNOWN remains explicit. Conditional mean bands do not guarantee the individual hidden latent class.

| learnable | previous_repaired | 14.7% | 86.3% | 37/2375 | 0/4 |

| misleading | previous_repaired | 14.7% | 86.3% | 37/2375 | 0/4 |

| null | previous_repaired | 0.0% | n/a | 0/2375 | 0/4 |

Reversal lag (first post126 alarm minus126): [17, 5, 7, 15]; previous repaired: [8, 9, 7, 11]. Repeated/nuisance alarm counts are in summary.csv. Stronger measurements can increase damage before an unannounced reversal is observed; inspect false-GREAT protection separately from stable coverage.

Prior diagnosis: no tier-dispatch bug or overlapping thresholds. The prior80% coarse sensor mixes states; the narrow0.4-percentage-point middle bands, at most21 recent independent date clusters, and train/calibration discrepancy produce intervals crossing boundaries. Global gates further suppress coverage. Exact counts and descriptive realized cell means are in report.json diagnosis; these are overlapping diagnostics, not causal ablation estimates.

Independent full post-BUY risk reconstruction: 8830 BUYs, maximum modeled entire-candidate loss 0.02000000 <=0.02. T+1 tested. No scale-up or terminal liquidation. Risk is modeled, not a realized-loss guarantee.

tier_aggregate.csv reports per-tier precision, coverage and forward realized net return. Confusion CSVs provide5×5 plus UNKNOWN, for latent EV and individual realized tiers. paired.csv and paired_aggregate.csv report matched return/MDD deltas against both comparators. Abstention costs are signed forgone net sum, positive realized sum and positive evaluator-EV sum per opportunity; these ex-post quantities ignore cash conflicts and are not attainable portfolio returns.

Reproduce from repository root (requires the original MTS checkout and sibling dependencies):
```bash
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration2/experiment.py > /tmp/discrimination_iteration2_execution.log 2>&1
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration2/finalize.py
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration2/audit_artifacts.py
```
Runner stops on any failed test. It overwrites outputs; archive this directory before reproduction. preregister.json is frozen and its execution hash is in report.json. source_checksums.json covers reviewed/executed sources, local corrected manager/kernel and patch are preserved, all6000 eligible outcome events per tape are in ledger archives, held-out scoring records in opportunities.jsonl.gz, actual executions in replays.jsonl.gz. run.log is the external task transcript; execution.log summarizes the test/replay timing and budget.

Missing data and limitations:
- Real PIT sensor time series with release timestamps, revision history, missingness, and measured noise covariance; injected Gaussian quality has no empirical realism validation.
- Longer independent matured stable-regime dates for narrow middle economic bands; independent calibration episodes and more forward seeds/regimes.
- Observable change predictors available before entry, or more rapid genuinely matured horizon outcomes to protect inversion before alarms.
- A holding-horizon-specific net-return label and causal selection-aware portfolio counterfactual for attainable abstention cost.
- Injected synthetic sensor quality is assumed, not calibrated to any real measurement; no real alpha claim.
- Conditional mean uncertainty is not individual latent-state certainty; neighboring classes overlap even with narrow intervals.
- Diagnostic SE intervals have no formal simultaneous or serial dependence coverage guarantee.
- Unannounced inversion cannot be detected before strictly matured outcomes; pre-alarm false GREAT unavoidable.
- Rolling online holdout ingests only matured realized returns, never evaluator truth; not a static untouched outcome fit.
- Four paired seeds,126 sessions; no statistical robustness claim.
- One-session DGP grosses up costs; multi-session portfolio holdings pay costs less often, null profits are not recognition.
- Abstention opportunity costs are ex-post sums per opportunity, not attainable portfolio returns.
- No terminal liquidation; modeled2% risk is not a realized-loss guarantee.
- Primary pooled estimator uses its recent calibration window in the point estimate. That window is a drift/stability diagnostic, not independent calibration. Independent static train0..77/cal80..121 ablation is reported separately and was reserved before execution.
- Primary initial rolling split is33..92 older,93..123 recent, as explicitly stated before execution; it does not use nominal static ranges.
- Static ablation radius formula is inherited unchanged from primary method, but static ablation has no rolling drift adaptation or residual gate and no portfolio replay; cannot certify the complete online bundle.

Additional artifact interpretation:

- Independent static ablation reaches55.3% stable coverage but fails every seed and causes2336 false GREAT at inversion; static mean confidence cannot certify temporal validity.
- Online stable scenarios emit only5 GOOD predictions each across four seeds; populated aggregate tiers are not reliable five-way identification.
- Stable tapes produce2..6 nuisance alarms per seed; quarantine and post-reset warmup remove large fractions of coverage. Detector scans five cells repeatedly without multiplicity calibration; nominal3SE comparisons do not control a sequential false-alarm rate.
- All online holdout decisions:13920 quarantine,9405 insufficient matured dates,7549 boundary uncertainty,9192 absent signal,32 residual-bias veto,7518 confident. These mutually exclusive prioritized reasons are not causal effect estimates.
- Future work requires independent sequential false-alarm calibration and better time-stamped change predictors. No post-holdout detector tuning was performed.
- Earlier radius attribution is descriptive of the repaired prior estimator:33812 middle-centered UNKNOWN intervals all have radius>=.002. Narrowing economic bands or counting stocks as independent would not be a valid fix.

### Existing local review (not Claude)
# Review and interpretation

Reviewed five_tier_discrimination and opportunity_evaluator_fix experiment.py, reports, preregistrations, tests and finalizers; scaling_robustness failure summary; original /opt/Momentum-Trading-System_v5-alpha/discovery/competitive_ga/functional_niche_manager.py; repaired snapshot causal_ledger.py. Paths and hashes are in source_checksums.json.

The original manager validates dated proposals, comparable EV units and funded execution. Quality is supplied by the conditional_ev callback: its contract cannot certify provenance or recognize five opportunity classes. The causal ledger verifies decision/outcome timestamps and immutability, but has no conditional return estimator. Its available() permits maturity <= cutoff, while this experiment conservatively requires maturity < cutoff.

The prior five-tier frozen estimator failed at inversion (989 false GREAT /2394 true TERRIBLE), while rolling repair reduced this to22 but removed nearly all classification. Neither result established middle-class identification. Previous null scaling experiments also showed confidently miscalibrated signals could increase tails: therefore no scaling expansion is attempted here.

The economic bands are disjoint. infer() supports every band and requires the full interval in one band. A middle band has width0.004 and half-width0.002. An80%-faithful coarse sensor plus20% independent random classes implies E[net|cell] approximately0.8*class_mean in the balanced stable DGP. Thus POOR/GOOD cells center near -0.0032/+0.0032, only0.0012 away from one boundary. A diagnostic radius above0.0012 alone makes those cells UNKNOWN. Date-common noise0.003 and idiosyncratic noise0.008, mixed class membership and at most21 recent cell-date clusters make such radii unsurprising. Counting all stocks as independent would incorrectly tighten uncertainty. Exact saved-record diagnostics in report.json distinguish middle-centered UNKNOWN, radius<0.002, intervals fitting a middle band despite other gates, and reasons; they overlap and are descriptive, not causal estimates.

Enhancement adds two Gaussian-noisy latent measurements, sd0.30/0.50 class units. Independent errors and known relative sensor variances are assumptions of the injected experiment. They are observable before execution by construction; neither is a label or future return. These assumptions have no demonstrated real-market realism. Null removes association and inversion flips the measurement relationship without an observable warning. Realized tape and costs are shared across the three policies. Comparison changes information and estimation jointly; there is no estimator-only causal claim.

Primary estimator uses six pseudo-date hierarchical shrinkage toward a learned linear parent and an isotonic fit oriented using matured data; inversion may learn descending order. Diagnostic intervals add sampling uncertainty, recent-window discrepancy and shrinkage displacement. Issued-forecast residuals are date clustered and available only after maturity. Drift compares old/recent cell-date means, quarantines all decisions and resets the history floor. UNKNOWN is retained for insufficient dates, absent identifiable spread, boundary uncertainty, quarantine and residual bias.

Protocol limitation: the primary recent window is pooled into the estimated mean. Its discrepancy is a stability diagnostic, not independent calibration. This was explicitly specified in the frozen method, but falls short of the requested independent-calibration standard. The reserved static ablation fits the estimate only on signals0..77, evaluates calibration80..121 and scores126..249 without refitting; it cannot replace causal rolling evaluation or certify the complete online algorithm. All result interpretation must retain this limitation; parameters were not repaired using holdout outcomes.

A stronger sensor can be confidently wrong immediately after an unannounced inversion. Detector latency and false GREAT must be checked against the repaired comparator on the same new tapes. Increased stable coverage or profitable portfolios cannot override this safety comparison. Diagnostic SE intervals are neither formal prediction intervals for latent classes nor distribution-free coverage guarantees. The experiment reports failure whenever the preregistered all-seed criteria fail.

se":0.0138743353206707,"slope":0.8070301300331262,"intercept":-0.00764623907286674,"rank_correlation":0.551565697809449}}],"exact_aggregate_matrices":[{"scenario":"learnable","variant":"iteration2","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[1011,4,0,0,0,1360],[25,198,12,0,0,2095],[0,9,307,1,0,2054],[0,0,9,4,27,2321],[0,0,0,0,1031,1436]],"realized_return_5x6":[[767,77,85,1,15,2675],[138,33,53,0,30,1222],[73,39,62,2,72,1315],[32,29,58,1,134,1188],[26,33,70,1,807,2866]]},{"scenario":"learnable","variant":"previous_repaired","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[472,0,0,0,37,1866],[27,0,0,0,30,2273],[18,0,0,0,52,2301],[15,0,0,0,36,2310],[25,0,0,0,1034,1408]],"realized_return_5x6":[[359,0,0,0,76,3185],[81,0,0,0,53,1342],[41,0,0,0,98,1424],[40,0,0,0,152,1250],[36,0,0,0,810,2957]]},{"scenario":"misleading","variant":"iteration2","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[1011,4,0,0,0,1360],[25,198,12,0,0,2095],[0,9,307,1,0,2054],[0,0,9,4,27,2321],[0,0,0,0,1031,1436]],"realized_return_5x6":[[767,77,85,1,15,2675],[138,33,53,0,30,1222],[73,39,62,2,72,1315],[32,29,58,1,134,1188],[26,33,70,1,807,2866]]},{"scenario":"misleading","variant":"previous_repaired","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[472,0,0,0,37,1866],[27,0,0,0,30,2273],[18,0,0,0,52,2301],[15,0,0,0,36,2310],[25,0,0,0,1034,1408]],"realized_return_5x6":[[359,0,0,0,76,3185],[81,0,0,0,53,1342],[41,0,0,0,98,1424],[40,0,0,0,152,1250],[36,0,0,0,810,2957]]},{"scenario":"null","variant":"iteration2","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,2375],[0,0,0,0,0,2330],[0,0,0,0,0,2371],[0,0,0,0,0,2361],[0,0,0,0,0,2467]],"realized_return_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,3620],[0,0,0,0,0,1476],[0,0,0,0,0,1563],[0,0,0,0,0,1442],[0,0,0,0,0,3803]]},{"scenario":"null","variant":"previous_repaired","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,2375],[0,0,
