### Method source experiment.py
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent injected-signal diagnostic; no production writes or oracle decision inputs."""
import sys,json,math,random,statistics as st,time,subprocess,gzip,csv,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix'))
import importlib.util
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('previous_evaluator',OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix/experiment.py')
b=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);sys.modules[spec.name]=b;spec.loader.exec_module(b)
sys.path.remove(str(OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix'))
m,k,r,s=b.m,b.k,b.r,b.s
TIERS,EDGES,tier,infer,observed=b.TIERS,b.EDGES,b.tier,b.infer,b.observed
sha=b.sha

def isotonic(values,weights):
    blocks=[]
    for i,(v,w) in enumerate(zip(values,weights)):
        blocks.append([i,i,v*w,w])
        while len(blocks)>1 and blocks[-2][2]/blocks[-2][3]>blocks[-1][2]/blocks[-1][3]:
            q=blocks.pop();p=blocks.pop();blocks.append([p[0],q[1],p[2]+q[2],p[3]+q[3]])
    result=[0.]*len(values)
    for lo,hi,v,w in blocks:result[lo:hi+1]=[v/w]*(hi-lo+1)
    return result

def generate(seed,scenario):
    """Truth separately returned for scoring. Sensors are injected noisy PIT measurements.

    Independent sensor RNG keeps tape outcomes identical across scenario variants.
    Sensor1 sd .30 and sensor2 sd .50 latent-class units; inverse-variance fusion.
    No observable change flag is provided at inversion.
    """
    rng=random.Random(seed);sensor=random.Random(seed+90000)
    coarse=[];features=[];pred=[];truth=[];rets=[];mu=(-.012,-.004,0.,.004,.012)
    for i in range(252):
        common=rng.gauss(0,.003);cc=[];ff=[];pp=[];tt=[];rr=[]
        for j in range(24):
            z=rng.randrange(5);c=z if rng.random()<.8 else rng.randrange(5)
            net=mu[z]+common+rng.gauss(0,.008)
            # Null: sensors and coarse feature independent of the outcome class.
            source=sensor.randrange(5) if scenario=='null' else z
            x=source+sensor.gauss(0,.30);y=source+sensor.gauss(0,.50)
            f=max(0,min(4,int(math.floor((x/.09+y/.25)/(1/.09+1/.25)+.5))))
            if scenario=='null':c=sensor.randrange(5)
            if scenario=='reversal' and i>=126:c=4-c;f=4-f
            prediction=.010-mu[c] if scenario in ('misleading','null','reversal') else mu[c]
            gross=(1+net)*(1+s.FRICTION)*(1+s.COSTS['fees'])/((1-s.FRICTION)*(1-s.COSTS['fees']))-1
            cc.append(c);ff.append(f);pp.append(prediction);tt.append(mu[z]);rr.append(gross)
        coarse.append(cc);features.append(ff);pred.append(pp);truth.append(tt);rets.append(rr)
    prices=[100.+j for j in range(24)];days=[]
    for i,d in enumerate(s.DATES):
        if i>=2:prices=[p*(1+v) for p,v in zip(prices,rets[i-2])]
        days.append((d,{t:k.Bar(prices[j],prices[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)}))
    return days,coarse,features,pred,truth

def estimate(grouped):
    raw=[st.mean(v) if v else 0. for v in grouped];weights=[max(1,len(v)) for v in grouped]
    center=sum(v*w for v,w in zip(raw,weights))/sum(weights)
    slope=sum((i-2)*(v-center)*w for i,v,w in zip(range(5),raw,weights))/sum((i-2)**2*w for i,w in enumerate(weights))
    # Hierarchical shrinkage: six pseudo dates toward the learned linear parent.
    shrunk=[(v*w+6*(center+slope*(i-2)))/(w+6) for i,(v,w) in enumerate(zip(raw,weights))]
    sign=1 if slope>=0 else -1
    return [sign*v for v in isotonic([sign*v for v in shrunk],weights)]

def predict(days,features):
    ledger=[];states=[];alarms=[];quarantine=-1;floor=0
    bysignal={};residuals={}
    for now in range(252):
        for row in bysignal.get(now-3,[]):
            try:row['realized_net']=observed(days,row['index'],row['j']);row['missingness']='OBSERVED';row['available_index']=now
            except (KeyError,IndexError,TypeError):row['missingness']='MISSING'
        matured=[z for i in range(max(floor,now-93),now-2) for z in bysignal.get(i,[]) if z['missingness']=='OBSERVED']
        grouped=[];dated=[];cal=[];drift=[]
        for c in range(5):
            dates={}
            for z in matured:
                if z['signal']==c:dates.setdefault(z['index'],[]).append(z['realized_net'])
            dv=[(i,st.mean(v)) for i,v in sorted(dates.items())];dated.append(dv)
            grouped.append([v for _,v in dv]);cal.append([v for i,v in dv if i>=now-33])
            old=[v for i,v in dv if i<now-11];recent=[v for i,v in dv if i>=now-11]
            if len(old)>=24 and len(recent)>=7 and abs(st.mean(old)-st.mean(recent))>max(.004,3*math.hypot(b.se(old),b.se(recent))):drift.append(c)
        if drift and now>quarantine:
            alarms.append(dict(index=now,cells=drift));quarantine=now+20;floor=now-10
        means=estimate(grouped);identifiable=max(means)-min(means)>=.003
        cells=[]
        for c in range(5):
            values=grouped[c];cv=cal[c];mean=means[c]
            bias=abs(st.mean(cv)-mean) if cv else 1.
            radius=max(2.58*b.se(values),bias+1.64*b.se(cv)) if values and cv else 1.
            # Shrinkage displacement is additional model uncertainty, never hidden.
            if values:radius+=abs(mean-st.mean(values))
            ntrain=sum(i<now-33 for i,v in dated[c]);ncal=len(cv)
            label=infer(mean,radius,ntrain,ncal)
            # Causal rolling interval reliability of forecasts as issued, date clustered.
            rv=[]
            for i in range(max(0,now-63),now-2):
                rows=[z for z in bysignal.get(i,[]) if z['signal']==c and z['missingness']=='OBSERVED' and z['forecast_ready']]
                if rows:rv.append(st.mean(z['realized_net']-z['inferred_net'] for z in rows))
            reliability=st.mean(abs(v)<=.006 for v in rv) if rv else 0.
            reason='CONFIDENT'
            if now<=quarantine:reason='DRIFT_QUARANTINE'
            elif not identifiable:reason='NO_IDENTIFIABLE_SIGNAL'
            elif ntrain<20 or ncal<20:reason='INSUFFICIENT_MATURE_DATES'
            elif len(rv)>=20 and abs(st.mean(rv))>max(.003,2.58*b.se(rv)):reason='UNRELIABLE_RESIDUAL_BIAS'
            elif label=='UNKNOWN':reason='BOUNDARY_UNCERTAINTY'
            if reason!='CONFIDENT':label='UNKNOWN'
            cells.append(dict(mean=mean,radius=radius,ntrain=ntrain,ncal=ncal,label=label,reason=reason,reliability=reliability,reliability_dates=len(rv),latest_outcome_signal=max((i for i,v in dated[c]),default=-3)))
        states.append(cells)
        if now<250:
            rows=[]
            for j in range(24):
                c=cells[features[now][j]]
                rows.append(dict(index=now,j=j,signal=features[now][j],execution_index=now+1,maturity_index=now+2,available_index=None,realized_net=None,missingness='PENDING',inferred_net=c['mean'],radius=c['radius'],inferred_tier=c['label'],reason=c['reason'],forecast_ready=min(c['ntrain'],c['ncal'])>=20))
            ledger.extend(rows);bysignal[now]=rows
    return states,ledger,alarms

def writecsv(name,rows):
    if not rows:return
    with (OUT/name).open('w') as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

def diagnose():
    """Prior saved outcomes only; descriptive attribution, no portfolio/tuning."""
    reasons={};cells={};unknown=0;center_middle=0;narrow=0;gate_middle=0
    p=OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix/opportunities.jsonl.gz'
    with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:
        for line in f:
            z=json.loads(line)
            if not 126<=z['index']<250:continue
            reason=z['reason'];reasons[reason]=reasons.get(reason,0)+1
            key=(z['scenario'],z['signal']);v=cells.setdefault(key,[])
            if z['realized_net'] is not None:v.append(z['realized_net'])
            if z['inferred_tier']=='UNKNOWN':
                unknown+=1
                if tier(z['inferred_net']) in TIERS[1:4]:
                    center_middle+=1
                    if z['radius']<.002:narrow+=1
                    if b.tier(z['inferred_net']-z['radius'])==b.tier(z['inferred_net']+z['radius']):gate_middle+=1
    return dict(prior_reasons=reasons,unknown=unknown,middle_center_unknown=center_middle,middle_center_radius_below_half_band=narrow,middle_interval_fits_but_gated=gate_middle,realized_cell_means={str(k):st.mean(v) for k,v in cells.items()},conclusions=['No tier dispatch bug: absolute infer emits all five tiers in unit tests. Bands are disjoint, not overlapping.','80% faithful coarse sensor mixes neighboring latent states; conditional cell EV is not individual latent EV.','Middle bands have half-width .002. Diagnostic radius and window bias dominate when center lies near boundaries.','All samples are date clustered: 24 stocks are not 24 independent dates. Prior recent calibration has at most 21 dates.','Global quarantine and signal-identifiability gates additionally suppress coverage; counters isolate these from interval rejection.','Original manager delegates quality to caller conditional_ev; causal ledger handles outcome availability, not inference validity.'])

def main():
    start=time.monotonic();plan=json.loads((OUT/'preregister.json').read_text());ph=sha(OUT/'preregister.json')
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','unittest','discover','-s',str(OUT),'-p','test_*.py','-v'],capture_output=True,text=True)
    (OUT/'tests.log').write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
    if result.returncode:
        (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='STOP_TEST_FAILURE',full_portfolio_replays=0)));raise SystemExit(result.returncode)
    sources=list(b.a.SOURCES)+[b.PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py',b.PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py',Path(b.__file__),OUT/'experiment.py',OUT/'test_iteration2.py',OUT/'preregister.json']
    sources+=list((OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination').glob('*.py'))
    sources+=[OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination/report.json',OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix/report.json',OUT.parent.parent/'mts_v5_manager_repair_20261031/v5_working_copy/engines/counterfactual_learning/causal_ledger.py']
    before={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources};diagnosis=diagnose();scores=[];metrics=[];attempts=0;opps=[];riskmax=0.;buys=0
    with gzip.open(OUT/'opportunities.jsonl.gz','wt') as of,gzip.open(OUT/'replays.jsonl.gz','wt') as rf:
        for seed in plan['seeds']:
            for scenario in plan['scenarios']:
                assert time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                days,coarse,f,p,truth=generate(seed,scenario)
                new,ledger,alarms=predict(days,f);previous,oldledger,oldalarms=b.causal_predictions(days,coarse,p)
                for variant,features,cells,als in [('iteration2',f,new,alarms),('previous_repaired',coarse,previous,oldalarms)]:
                    score,rows=b.discrimination(days,features,p,truth,cells)
                    score.pop('cells',None)
                    scores.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,alarms=als,reversal_lag=next((z['index']-126 for z in als if z['index']>=126),None) if scenario=='reversal' else None,**score))
                    for z in rows:
                        z.update(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant);of.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                    for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'):
                        v=[z for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']==t]
                        opps.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,tier=t,n=len(v),coverage=len(v)/len(rows),precision=st.mean(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in v) if v and t!='UNKNOWN' else None,realized_net=st.mean(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if v else None,abstained_positive_net_sum=sum(max(0,z['realized_net']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_signed_net_sum=sum(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_positive_ev_sum=sum(max(0,z['true_ev']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0))
                with gzip.open(OUT/f'ledger_{seed}_{scenario}.jsonl.gz','wt') as lf:
                    for z in ledger:lf.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                for variant,features,cells,policy in [('iteration2',f,new,'tier10'),('previous_repaired',coarse,previous,'tier10'),('existing_ev10',coarse,previous,'existing_ev10')]:
                    assert attempts<plan['max_full_replays'] and time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                    attempts+=1;(OUT/'attempts.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(attempts=attempts,seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant)))
                    kw=b.callbacks(features,p,cells,policy,days);evdays=days[126:252]
                    candidates=[m.Candidate(t,'GA7','synthetic',.02,s.GAPS[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)]
                    result=m.run_slice(evdays,candidates,max_sessions=126,max_stocks=24,**s.COSTS,**kw)
                    metric,_=s.measure(result,evdays);metric['total_return']=metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL-1
                    metric['cagr']=(metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL)**2-1
                    metrics.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,**metric))
                    # Independent full post-BUY position risk, reconstructed from actual fills/NAV.
                    previous_shares={};bydate={d:[] for d,_ in evdays}
                    for fill in result['ledger']:bydate[fill[0]].append(fill)
                    for (d,bars),nav in zip(evdays,result['nav']):
                        fills=bydate[d];opening=nav[1]+sum(z[5]+z[3]*abs(z[4]-bars[z[1]].open) for z in fills)
                        for z in fills:
                            if z[2]=='BUY':
                                loss=(previous_shares.get(z[1],0)+z[3])*bars[z[1]].open/opening*(max(.02,s.GAPS[int(z[1][3:])])+s.ROUND_TRIP)
                                assert loss<=.02+1e-10;buys+=1;riskmax=max(riskmax,loss)
                        previous_shares=nav[3]
                    rf.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,ledger=result['ledger'],nav=result['nav'],risk=result['risk_audit'],decisions=result['decisions']))+'\n')
                print(f'{seed} {scenario} {attempts}/{plan["max_full_replays"]} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for z in metrics:
        if z['variant']!='iteration2':continue
        for comparator in ('previous_repaired','existing_ev10'):
            old=next(v for v in metrics if v['seed']==z['seed'] and v['scenario']==z['scenario'] and v['variant']==comparator)
            paired.append(dict(seed=z['seed'],scenario=z['scenario'],comparator=comparator,return_delta=z['total_return']-old['total_return'],mdd_delta=z['mdd']-old['mdd'],iteration2_return=z['total_return'],comparator_return=old['total_return']))
    assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in before.items());assert sha(OUT/'preregister.json')==ph
    for score in scores:
        with (OUT/f'confusion_{score["seed"]}_{score["scenario"]}_{score["variant"]}.csv').open('w') as cf:
            w=csv.writer(cf);w.writerow(['true / predicted',*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'])
            for i,t in enumerate(TIERS):w.writerow([t,*score['confusion_ev'][i],score['unknown_by_ev_tier'][i]])
    writecsv('tier_metrics.csv',opps);writecsv('portfolio_runs.csv',metrics);writecsv('paired.csv',paired)
    report=dict(status='PASS' if all(z['status']=='PASS' for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration2' and z['scenario']!='null') and all(z['coverage']==0 for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration2' and z['scenario']=='null') else 'FAIL',execution_status='COMPLETE',full_portfolio_replays=attempts,elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,preregister_sha256=ph,source_sha256=before,production_sources_unchanged=True,diagnosis=diagnosis,discrimination=scores,portfolio_runs=metrics,paired=paired,tier_metrics=opps,independent_risk_audit=dict(actual_buys=buys,max_candidate_modeled_loss=riskmax,passed=True),limitations=plan['limitations'])
    (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(report['status'])
if __name__=='__main__':main()

```

## Iteration 3 — discrimination_iteration3
### README.md
# Discrimination iteration3 — FAIL

Completed 48/48 paired portfolio replays in 380.7s. Tests passed before replays; see tests.log. No GA or production modifications.

Frozen train signals 0–77; independent calibration 80–121; embargo; holdout 126–249. Calibration never changes point estimates. Holdout never changes estimates, uncertainty, or posterior. Matured holdout contrast blocks feed only a permanent safety veto. These exact synthetic tapes were already viewed in iteration2: this is a regression experiment, not new confirmatory evidence.

Fit uses clipped cell date means, neighboring-cell partial pooling and five-date block standard errors. Calibration adds independent residual bias and sampling uncertainty. Economic boundaries remain −0.6%, −0.2%, +0.2%, +0.6%; the entire interval must fit a band and Gaussian conditional-mean posterior must exceed .95. UNKNOWN remains explicit. Gaussian block approximations assume independent/stable blocks; coverage is not distribution-free or certified with eight calibration blocks.

| Scenario | Evaluator | Coverage | Accuracy | False GREAT / TERRIBLE | Monotone seeds |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| learnable | iteration3 | 35.2% | 97.2% | 0/2375 | 0/4 |
| learnable | iteration2_repaired | 22.2% | 96.7% | 0/2375 | 1/4 |
| misleading | iteration3 | 35.2% | 97.2% | 0/2375 | 0/4 |
| misleading | iteration2_repaired | 22.2% | 96.7% | 0/2375 | 1/4 |
| null | iteration3 | 0.0% | n/a | 0/2375 | 0/4 |
| null | iteration2_repaired | 0.0% | n/a | 0/2375 | 0/4 |
| reversal | iteration3 | 2.1% | 0.0% | 102/2375 | 0/4 |
| reversal | iteration2_repaired | 18.8% | 93.8% | 23/2375 | 1/4 |

GOOD/AVERAGE/POOR support, every class precision/recall and realized net means are in tier_aggregate.csv and report.json. Full 5×5 matrices plus UNKNOWN are in confusion_aggregate_*_{ev,realized}.csv. Portfolio CAGR/MDD/exposure/costs and paired differences are in portfolio_runs.csv, portfolio_aggregate.csv and paired*.csv. Exact per-seed results remain in report.json.

Reversal lags: [7, 7, 7, 7]; iteration2: [17, 5, 7, 15]. Alarm counts are in summary.csv. A latch cannot anticipate inversion; it never relearns from holdout.

Stable synthetic sensor contains predictive information by construction; failure to certify middle bands with 78/42 dates and block uncertainty is finite-information limitation, not proof of no signal. Null lacks PIT association by construction. Reversal has no observable advance warning: before matured outcomes no causal detector can distinguish it. Real PIT predictive information remains untested; no real-market edge inferred.

Iteration2 diagnosis: short rolling windows and sparse per-cell independent dates inflate error; calibration pooled into mean invalidates independence; rolling residual vetoes and unadjusted repeated cell scans suppress coverage and produce nuisance alarms. Iteration3 separates those mechanisms but cannot manufacture missing information. Full shrinkage displacement and independent calibration uncertainty are retained instead of shrinking intervals merely to populate bins.

Robust all-seed pass gates are frozen in preregister.json. Portfolio returns cannot override discriminator failure. No real-market edge claim. Modeled candidate risk <=2%, T+1 and cash/cost contracts tested; realized losses are not guaranteed capped. No terminal liquidation; one-session outcome label does not certify multi-session portfolio EV.

Reproduce from repository root (requires original MTS checkout and sibling dependencies):
```bash
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration3/experiment.py > /tmp/iteration3_execution.log 2>&1
```
Runner stops at test failure. It overwrites artifacts; archive first. finalize.py only aggregates saved results. Original native manager delegates quality to conditional_ev; the causal ledger enforces availability rather than estimating quality. Dependencies and source hashes are retained. Ledger archives contain all eligible opportunities, including unselected entries; scoring truth is added only by evaluator after forecasts.

Limitations:
- Synthetic regression tapes previously evaluated in iteration2: untouched means no fit/update with holdout outcomes in iteration3, not fresh confirmatory data.
- Five-date block independence and approximately Gaussian date-block means assumed; finite sample robust coverage is not guaranteed.
- Posterior is over conditional mean bands, not individual latent class; no oracle labels in fit.
- A shock before matured outcomes cannot be predicted by a safety alarm.
- Calibration uncertainty may honestly preclude middle tiers; no thresholds relaxed.
- Synthetic sensor information is injected; no real-market alpha claim.
- One-session net labels differ from multi-session portfolio holdings; no terminal liquidation.

Detailed saved-result audit and paired enrichment follow.

Stable synthetic sensor contains predictive information by construction; failure to certify middle bands with 78/42 dates and block uncertainty is finite-information limitation, not proof of no signal. Null lacks PIT association by construction. Reversal has no observable advance warning: before matured outcomes no causal detector can distinguish it. Real PIT predictive information remains untested; no real-market edge inferred. Stable middle radii .00236..00508 exceed .002 half-band: all middle support remains zero, so five-way monotonicity fails on every seed. Stable coverage rises by 13.03 percentage points through extreme classes only. Stable nuisance alarms decline from 15 to 1 across four seeds, but permanent latching sacrifices adaptation. At reversal, stronger frozen extreme assignments cause 102 false GREAT before the seven-session alarm versus23 online comparator; pre-alarm wrong assignments explain zero reversal conditional accuracy. This separates stationary finite-information limits from unavoidable delayed regime evidence; it does not establish that predictive information is universally absent.

### Existing local review (not Claude)
# Review and inference limits

Reviewed iteration2 README, preregistration, full report (including static ablation, follow-up diagnosis, per-seed scores and ledgers), experiment/test/finalizer scripts, opportunity_evaluator_fix inference/callback/replay implementation, original native functional_niche_manager, and the repaired causal ledger. Imported dependencies are hashed in source_checksums.json. The local manager/kernel/patch snapshots preserve the inherited execution seam; production sources are unchanged.

The native manager requires causal, comparable cost-adjusted conditional_ev from its caller. It cannot establish measurement provenance or a five-class quality model. The prior causal ledger enforces immutable timestamped outcomes and availability; availability alone does not establish independent calibration or predictive information.

Iteration2 primary estimates pooled recent calibration observations into their point estimates. Its separate static ablation had independent splits but retained an uncertainty formula inherited from the online model and lacked portfolio replay or drift protection. Sparse per-cell dates, random class mixing, date-common noise, short windows and repeated nuisance alarms explain middle abstention without any tier-dispatch bug.

Iteration3 keeps all sensor inputs, seeds, costs and tapes unchanged. Training uses date means clipped at +/-5% (bounded influence against extreme return contamination), aggregated into fixed five-date blocks. Two pseudo blocks pool toward adjacent-cell training means; the entire shrinkage displacement remains an uncertainty penalty. Calibration uses only independent chronological residual blocks to enlarge intervals, not alter point estimates. No stock-level independence is assumed. This deliberately modest pooling cannot remove calibration sample uncertainty or learn absent information.

There are only 16 effective training blocks and eight calibration blocks. Normal approximations and the fixed correlation block length are assumptions, not finite-sample guarantees. The interval targets a stationary conditional mean for a measurement cell, not a future individual return. The ordinal posterior integrates that approximate conditional-mean distribution over the fixed economic bands. It is not a calibrated probability of a hidden individual generative class: that class is evaluator-only, and cannot be calibrated against oracle labels. Confusion against hidden EV tiers and noisy realized tiers are distinct outputs.

The holdout monitor is safety-only and consumes strictly matured high-minus-low outcome contrasts on fixed nonoverlapping five-date blocks. It cancels additive date-common shocks, reduces multiplicity to 26 prespecified looks, and latches abstention. Its Gaussian union-bound threshold includes baseline uncertainty; estimated variance, serial dependence and heavy tails prevent a distribution-free sequential false-alarm claim. No post-alarm relearning is permitted, so loss of coverage under persistent drift is intentional. An inversion with no advance observable warning is causally indistinguishable before outcomes mature.

The four synthetic tapes were already evaluated in iteration2. Iteration3 holds outcomes out from fitting/calibration but does not create a fresh confirmatory test set. No tuning after any iteration3 holdout scores. No empirical real-market PIT signals, release/revision histories, sensor covariance or independent regime episodes are supplied. Stable synthetic association is planted by construction; a failure here cannot prove real-world absence of alpha, and a pass would not establish real-market edge.

":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[1011,4,0,0,0,1360],[25,198,12,0,0,2095],[0,9,307,1,0,2054],[0,0,9,4,27,2321],[0,0,0,0,1031,1436]],"realized_return_5x6":[[767,77,85,1,15,2675],[138,33,53,0,30,1222],[73,39,62,2,72,1315],[32,29,58,1,134,1188],[26,33,70,1,807,2866]]},{"scenario":"null","variant":"iteration3","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,2375],[0,0,0,0,0,2330],[0,0,0,0,0,2371],[0,0,0,0,0,2361],[0,0,0,0,0,2467]],"realized_return_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,3620],[0,0,0,0,0,1476],[0,0,0,0,0,1563],[0,0,0,0,0,1442],[0,0,0,0,0,3803]]},{"scenario":"null","variant":"iteration2_repaired","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,2375],[0,0,0,0,0,2330],[0,0,0,0,0,2371],[0,0,0,0,0,2361],[0,0,0,0,0,2467]],"realized_return_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,0,3620],[0,0,0,0,0,1476],[0,0,0,0,0,1563],[0,0,0,0,0,1442],[0,0,0,0,0,3803]]},{"scenario":"reversal","variant":"iteration3","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[0,0,0,0,102,2273],[0,0,0,0,3,2327],[0,0,0,0,0,2371],[4,0,0,0,0,2357],[141,0,0,0,0,2326]],"realized_return_5x6":[[2,0,0,0,75,3543],[5,0,0,0,13,1458],[12,0,0,0,10,1541],[18,0,0,0,5,1419],[108,0,0,0,2,3693]]},{"scenario":"reversal","variant":"iteration2_repaired","seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"n_opportunities":11904,"latent_ev_5x6":[[904,1,0,0,23,1447],[25,75,5,0,1,2224],[0,3,143,1,0,2224],[0,10,5,7,28,2311],[35,1,0,0,975,1456]],"realized_return_5x6":[[699,38,34,2,38,2809],[109,16,27,0,40,1284],[71,15,30,3,74,1370],[37,10,19,2,113,1261],[48,11,43,1,762,2938]]}],"denominators":{"seeds":[3101,3102,3103,3104],"opportunities_per_seed_scenario_variant":[2976],"classification_scenario_variant_groups":8,"portfolio_replays":48,"paired_rows":32,"paired_seed_counts_by_scenario_and_comparator":{"('learnable', ### Method source experiment.py
```python
"""Iteration3: frozen conditional EV fit; independent calibration; safety-only holdout monitor."""
import sys,json,math,statistics as st,time,subprocess,gzip,csv,hashlib,importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('iteration2_reference',OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/experiment.py')
previous_model=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);sys.modules[spec.name]=previous_model;spec.loader.exec_module(previous_model)
b=previous_model.b
m,k,r,s=b.m,b.k,b.r,b.s
TIERS,EDGES,tier,infer,observed,sha=b.TIERS,b.EDGES,b.tier,b.infer,b.observed,b.sha
generate=previous_model.generate

def block_stats(pairs):
    groups={}
    for i,v in pairs:groups.setdefault(i//5,[]).append(v)
    values=[st.mean(v) for v in groups.values() if len(v)>=3]
    return (st.mean(values),st.stdev(values)/len(values)**.5,len(values)) if len(values)>1 else (0.,1.,len(values))

def date_values(days,features,lo,hi):
    result=[]
    for i in range(lo,hi):
        row=[]
        for c in range(5):
            vals=[]
            for j in range(24):
                if features[i][j]==c:
                    try:vals.append(observed(days,i,j))
                    except (KeyError,IndexError,TypeError):pass
            row.append(max(-.05,min(.05,st.mean(vals))) if vals else None)
        result.append((i,row))
    return result

def fit(days,features):
    train=date_values(days,features,0,78);cal=date_values(days,features,80,122)
    stats=[block_stats([(i,v[c]) for i,v in train if v[c] is not None]) for c in range(5)]
    raw=[v[0] for v in stats];means=[]
    for c in range(5):
        neighbors=[raw[h] for h in (c-1,c+1) if 0<=h<5]
        parent=st.mean(neighbors);n=stats[c][2]
        means.append((raw[c]*n+2*parent)/(n+2))
    cells=[]
    for c in range(5):
        residuals=[(i,v[c]-means[c]) for i,v in cal if v[c] is not None]
        bias,cse,ncal=block_stats(residuals);tse=stats[c][1]
        scale=math.hypot(tse,cse);penalty=abs(means[c]-raw[c])
        radius=abs(bias)+2.58*scale+penalty
        normal=st.NormalDist(means[c],max(scale+(abs(bias)+penalty)/2.58,1e-10))
        cdf=[0.]+[normal.cdf(x) for x in EDGES]+[1.]
        posterior=[cdf[i+1]-cdf[i] for i in range(5)]
        label=tier(means[c]) if tier(means[c]-radius)==tier(means[c]+radius) and max(posterior)>=.95 and stats[c][2]>=12 and ncal>=8 else 'UNKNOWN'
        cells.append(dict(mean=means[c],radius=radius,ntrain=sum(v[c] is not None for i,v in train),ncal=sum(v[c] is not None for i,v in cal),train_blocks=stats[c][2],calibration_blocks=ncal,train_se=tse,calibration_se=cse,calibration_bias=bias,shrinkage_penalty=penalty,posterior=posterior,label=label,reason='CONFIDENT' if label!='UNKNOWN' else 'BOUNDARY_UNCERTAINTY',latest_outcome_signal=121,reliability=0.))
    identifiable=max(means)-min(means)-2.58*math.hypot(stats[0][1],stats[4][1])>.003
    if not identifiable:
        for cell in cells:cell.update(label='UNKNOWN',reason='NO_IDENTIFIABLE_SIGNAL')
    contrast=[(i,v[4]-v[0]) for i,v in cal if v[0] is not None and v[4] is not None]
    baseline,se,n=block_stats(contrast)
    return cells,dict(baseline=baseline,block_sd=se*math.sqrt(n),baseline_se=se,blocks=n)

def predict(days,features):
    states=[];ledger=[];alarms=[];pending={};cells=None;monitor=None;contrasts=[];latched=False
    for now in range(252):
        for row in pending.get(now-3,[]):
            try:row.update(realized_net=observed(days,row['index'],row['j']),missingness='OBSERVED',available_index=now)
            except (KeyError,IndexError,TypeError):row['missingness']='MISSING'
        if now==126:cells,monitor=fit(days,features)
        # 26 prespecified nonoverlapping five-date looks. No fitted model update.
        if cells is not None and now>=133 and (now-133)%5==0 and not latched:
            start=now-7
            dv=date_values(days,features,start,start+5)
            vals=[v[4]-v[0] for i,v in dv if v[4] is not None and v[0] is not None]
            threshold=max(.004,st.NormalDist().inv_cdf(1-.01/(2*26))*math.hypot(monitor['block_sd'],monitor['baseline_se']))
            if len(vals)>=3 and abs(st.mean(vals)-monitor['baseline'])>threshold:
                alarms.append(dict(index=now,cells=[0,4],threshold=threshold,last_signal=now-3));latched=True
        current=[dict(c) for c in cells] if cells else [dict(mean=0.,radius=1.,ntrain=0,ncal=0,label='UNKNOWN',reason='PRE_FIT',latest_outcome_signal=-3,reliability=0.,posterior=[.2]*5) for _ in range(5)]
        if latched:
            for c in current:c.update(label='UNKNOWN',reason='DRIFT_LATCH')
        states.append(current)
        if now<250:
            rows=[]
            for j in range(24):
                c=current[features[now][j]]
                rows.append(dict(index=now,j=j,ticker=s.TICKERS[j],signal=features[now][j],decision_timestamp=s.DATES[now],execution_index=now+1,maturity_index=now+2,available_index=None,realized_net=None,missingness='PENDING',inferred_net=c['mean'],radius=c['radius'],inferred_tier=c['label'],reason=c['reason'],posterior=c['posterior'],split='train' if now<=77 else 'calibration' if 80<=now<=121 else 'holdout' if now>=126 else 'embargo',horizon='open(k+1) to open(k+2)',costs=dict(s.COSTS),friction=s.FRICTION))
            ledger.extend(rows);pending[now]=rows
    return states,ledger,alarms

def writecsv(name,rows):
    if rows:
        with (OUT/name).open('w') as f:
            w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

def diagnose():
    prior=json.loads((OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/report.json').read_text())
    return dict(prior_diagnosis=prior['diagnosis'],prior_ledger_audit=prior['ledger_audit'],conclusions=['Short recent cell windows confound estimation and calibration; date-common noise forbids stock count as independent N.','Frozen partial pooling uses neighboring cells only from training; independent block residuals calibrate uncertainty without fitting mean.','Five-date blocks address short date correlation but reduce effective sample. Missing cell dates reduce support further.','No holdout online fit or residual bias fit: safety latches independently, never narrows intervals.','No observable precursors to reversal in DGP; pre-maturation protection is information-limited.','Original manager accepts caller conditional_ev; it does not provide a class estimator. Prior causal ledger certifies timestamps, not calibration.'])
def main():
    start=time.monotonic();plan=json.loads((OUT/'preregister.json').read_text());ph=sha(OUT/'preregister.json')
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','unittest','discover','-s',str(OUT),'-p','test_*.py','-v'],capture_output=True,text=True)
    (OUT/'tests.log').write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
    if result.returncode:
        (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='STOP_TEST_FAILURE',full_portfolio_replays=0)));raise SystemExit(result.returncode)
    sources=list(b.a.SOURCES)+[b.PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py',b.PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py',Path(b.__file__),OUT/'experiment.py',OUT/'test_iteration3.py',OUT/'preregister.json']
    sources+=list((OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination').glob('*.py'))
    sources+=[OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination/report.json',OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix/report.json',OUT.parent.parent/'mts_v5_manager_repair_20261031/v5_working_copy/engines/counterfactual_learning/causal_ledger.py']
    sources += [Path(previous_model.__file__), OUT/'finalize.py', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/report.json', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/preregister.json', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/README.md']
    before={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources};diagnosis=diagnose();scores=[];metrics=[];attempts=0;opps=[];riskmax=0.;buys=0
    with gzip.open(OUT/'opportunities.jsonl.gz','wt') as of,gzip.open(OUT/'replays.jsonl.gz','wt') as rf:
        for seed in plan['seeds']:
            for scenario in plan['scenarios']:
                assert time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                days,coarse,f,p,truth=generate(seed,scenario)
                new,ledger,alarms=predict(days,f);previous,oldledger,oldalarms=previous_model.predict(days,f)
                for variant,features,cells,als in [('iteration3',f,new,alarms),('iteration2_repaired',f,previous,oldalarms)]:
                    score,rows=b.discrimination(days,features,p,truth,cells)
                    score.pop('cells',None)
                    scores.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,alarms=als,reversal_lag=next((z['index']-126 for z in als if z['index']>=126),None) if scenario=='reversal' else None,**score))
                    for z in rows:
                        z.update(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant);of.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                    for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'):
                        v=[z for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']==t]
                        opps.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,tier=t,n=len(v),coverage=len(v)/len(rows),precision=st.mean(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in v) if v and t!='UNKNOWN' else None,realized_net=st.mean(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if v else None,abstained_positive_net_sum=sum(max(0,z['realized_net']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_signed_net_sum=sum(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_positive_ev_sum=sum(max(0,z['true_ev']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0))
                with gzip.open(OUT/f'ledger_{seed}_{scenario}.jsonl.gz','wt') as lf:
                    for z in ledger:lf.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                for variant,features,cells,policy in [('iteration3',f,new,'tier10'),('iteration2_repaired',f,previous,'tier10'),('existing_ev10',coarse,previous,'existing_ev10')]:
                    assert attempts<plan['max_full_replays'] and time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                    attempts+=1;(OUT/'attempts.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(attempts=attempts,seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant)))
                    kw=b.callbacks(features,p,cells,policy,days);evdays=days[126:252]
                    candidates=[m.Candidate(t,'GA7','synthetic',.02,s.GAPS[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)]
                    result=m.run_slice(evdays,candidates,max_sessions=126,max_stocks=24,**s.COSTS,**kw)
                    metric,_=s.measure(result,evdays);metric['total_return']=metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL-1
                    metric['cagr']=(metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL)**2-1
                    metric['fees']=sum(z[5] for z in result['ledger'])
                    metric['spread_slippage_impact_cost']=sum(z[3]*abs(z[4]-dict(evdays)[z[0]][z[1]].open) for z in result['ledger'])
                    assert all(z[2]>=-1e-8 for z in result['nav'])
                    metrics.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,**metric))
                    # Independent full post-BUY position risk, reconstructed from actual fills/NAV.
                    previous_shares={};bydate={d:[] for d,_ in evdays}
                    for fill in result['ledger']:bydate[fill[0]].append(fill)
                    for (d,bars),nav in zip(evdays,result['nav']):
                        fills=bydate[d];opening=nav[1]+sum(z[5]+z[3]*abs(z[4]-bars[z[1]].open) for z in fills)
                        for z in fills:
                            if z[2]=='BUY':
                                loss=(previous_shares.get(z[1],0)+z[3])*bars[z[1]].open/opening*(max(.02,s.GAPS[int(z[1][3:])])+s.ROUND_TRIP)
                                assert loss<=.02+1e-10;buys+=1;riskmax=max(riskmax,loss)
                        previous_shares=nav[3]
                    rf.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,ledger=result['ledger'],nav=result['nav'],risk=result['risk_audit'],decisions=result['decisions']))+'\n')
                print(f'{seed} {scenario} {attempts}/{plan["max_full_replays"]} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for z in metrics:
        if z['variant']!='iteration3':continue
        for comparator in ('iteration2_repaired','existing_ev10'):
            old=next(v for v in metrics if v['seed']==z['seed'] and v['scenario']==z['scenario'] and v['variant']==comparator)
            paired.append(dict(seed=z['seed'],scenario=z['scenario'],comparator=comparator,return_delta=z['total_return']-old['total_return'],mdd_delta=z['mdd']-old['mdd'],iteration3_return=z['total_return'],comparator_return=old['total_return']))
    assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in before.items());assert sha(OUT/'preregister.json')==ph
    for score in scores:
        with (OUT/f'confusion_{score["seed"]}_{score["scenario"]}_{score["variant"]}.csv').open('w') as cf:
            w=csv.writer(cf);w.writerow(['true / predicted',*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'])
            for i,t in enumerate(TIERS):w.writerow([t,*score['confusion_ev'][i],score['unknown_by_ev_tier'][i]])
    writecsv('tier_metrics.csv',opps);writecsv('portfolio_runs.csv',metrics);writecsv('paired.csv',paired)
    report=dict(status='PASS' if all(z['status']=='PASS' for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration3' and z['scenario']!='null') and all(z['coverage']==0 for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration3' and z['scenario']=='null') else 'FAIL',execution_status='COMPLETE',full_portfolio_replays=attempts,elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,preregister_sha256=ph,source_sha256=before,production_sources_unchanged=True,diagnosis=diagnosis,discrimination=scores,portfolio_runs=metrics,paired=paired,tier_metrics=opps,independent_risk_audit=dict(actual_buys=buys,max_candidate_modeled_loss=riskmax,passed=True),limitations=plan['limitations'])
    (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(report['status'])
if __name__=='__main__':
    main()
    import finalize
    finalize.main()
```

## Iteration 4 — discrimination_iteration4
### README.md
# Discrimination iteration 4

Independent experiment; iteration3 calibration is rejected as deployment baseline. Iteration2's unchanged predictor and corrected local replay kernel are the comparator. No production edits or GA.

Run from repository root:
```
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration4/experiment.py > experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration4/execution.log 2>&1
python3 experiments/mts_v5_planted_signal_20261101/discrimination_iteration4/summarize.py
```
Requires the existing sibling MTS dependencies used by iteration2. Runner executes contract tests before replays and stops on failure. Reproduction overwrites artifacts; archive first.

Preregistration freezes fresh seeds 841729/926183 before tape generation, chronological train20..77/cal80..121/holdout126..249, fixed economic boundaries, model penalty, gate thresholds and 32 replay budget. No selection among ablations. Original iteration2 generator is unchanged: all policies receive identical price tapes, noisy sensors and costs. Full candidate, ungated full candidate, sensor-only and price-only models receive classification evaluation; iteration2, gated base, ungated base and gated scaled allocation receive paired portfolio evaluation. Scaling uses the existing additional realized-evidence requirements and 15% target, with cost-inclusive entire-position risk <=2%.

PIT predictors use only closes through the decision date: 20-session mean return minus fixed six-symbol synthetic sector proxy; mean minus market mean; mean divided by realized volatility; volatility. Sector membership is a fixed modeling proxy, not claimed real industry metadata. Economic hypotheses are relative strength persistence, idiosyncratic trend persistence and risk normalization. Their noise is the observed noisy return history; no fabricated signal or latent class access is added. Existing synthetic class sensor has injected Gaussian sd .30/.50 class units and retains its original inverse-variance fusion. Full ridge model adds price features; sensor-only and price-only ablations expose their contribution. Volume confirmation and revisions are unavailable and explicitly null in ledgers. No real fundamentals are inferred.

Forecast fit uses matured outcomes available by126 and never updates on holdout. Independent calibration provides date-block residual bias and uncertainty. The pooled interval is a diagnostic approximation, not a valid conditional or simultaneous coverage certificate: it does not model coefficient uncertainty, conditional heteroskedasticity or arbitrary serial dependence. Thresholds are not adjusted to populate tiers. UNKNOWN maps to NO_TRADE/exit through existing callbacks. Calibration does not fit point estimates.

Reliability is a separate policy function. It vetoes when the three models disagree by >.004, recent six-date matured forecast residual mean exceeds .004 in magnitude (minimum three dates), or price features depart >4 training SD. Deterioration/drift latch permanently; disagreement is per opportunity. The same gated state supplies GREAT confirmation and scaling. Gate outcomes never narrow intervals or force classes. Unannounced inversion has no advance observable marker; a matured-residual detector necessarily lags. Tests include a controlled reversal timing fixture; actual observed tape alarm lags are reported separately.

The original synthetic classes are independent across dates, so technical history has no designed predictive association with the next class. This is an intentional information test, not an attempt to inject a stronger oracle. Learnable and misleading scenarios have identical sensor/truth relationships; misleading refers to the legacy raw forecast. Null removes sensor association. Reversal inverts sensors at126 without a warning flag. No holdout outcomes are used to choose a model, threshold or seed.

Portfolio CAGR annualizes126 sessions; costs, T+1, cash and MDD behavior come from the unchanged corrected local kernel. One-session forward labels differ from multi-session holdings and positions are not terminally liquidated. Modeled 2% loss is not a realized-loss cap. Results are synthetic diagnostics only.

Noise/observability detail: the unchanged DGP adds .008 idiosyncratic and .003 date-common Gaussian net-return noise. Technical features inherit that noise and overlapping-window dependence; their three transformations are correlated and are not independent observations. Prices contain the cost gross-up in the original generator. Fixed six-symbol groups proxy sectors solely for this synthetic diagnostic. A missing price is mapped to zero return in the feature extractor (not proof of flat price); future-price truncation is tested for PIT causality, while missing-data robustness is not certified. The original injected sensor is observed at the decision index and has no actual market release record. It is retained only as a clearly named synthetic benchmark.




## Frozen holdout result: FAIL

FAIL: gate abstains on every holdout opportunity; zero false GREAT provides no evidence of useful discrimination. Full and sensor-only stable coverage differ by <.04 percentage points; price features add no demonstrated predictive value. Reversal latch lags 11/15 are deterioration/feature-drift alarms, not validated timely reversal detection. Global residual averaging can cancel opposing class errors; disagreement with an uninformative price-only model suppresses useful extreme forecasts. Scaling unexercised: zero incremental value is not evidence that active scaling is safe.

Completed 32 replays in 503.9s; pre-replay contract tests passed. No post-holdout tuning.

| Scenario | Model | Coverage | Accuracy | False GREAT / TERRIBLE |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| learnable | iteration4 | 0.0% | n/a | 0/1176 |
| learnable | iteration2_repaired | 23.8% | 97.5% | 0/1176 |
| learnable | ungated | 39.6% | 97.8% | 0/1176 |
| learnable | sensor_only | 39.7% | 97.8% | 0/1176 |
| learnable | price_only | 0.0% | n/a | 0/1176 |
| misleading | iteration4 | 0.0% | n/a | 0/1176 |
| misleading | iteration2_repaired | 23.8% | 97.5% | 0/1176 |
| misleading | ungated | 39.6% | 97.8% | 0/1176 |

Five-tier support, precision/recall and realized forward net means: tier_aggregate.csv. Complete latent/realized confusion matrices include UNKNOWN. Actual alarm lags (null means no latch) are in summary.csv; these are not guaranteed inversion detection. Gate reason counters describe instantaneous checks; an existing permanent latch can keep a decision UNKNOWN after those checks clear. Portfolio CAGR/MDD/costs: portfolio_aggregate.csv; per-seed matched allocation deltas: paired.csv (scaled comparator delta is base minus scaled).

The candidate fails robust discrimination requirements; low false-GREAT counts achieved through abstention do not establish useful classification. Price-only and ungated ablations show the information and safety tradeoff; portfolio returns do not override failed discrimination.

Next engineering blocker: Acquire release-timestamped real PIT candidate features with measured conditional predictive information and independent regime episodes; validate conditional uncertainty and pre-entry change indicators before scaling. OHLC history cannot forecast iid synthetic future classes; sensor/price agreement is insufficient certification.

### Method source experiment.py
```python
from model import *
def writecsv(name,rows):
    if rows:
        with (OUT/name).open('w') as f:
            w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

def diagnose():
    prior=json.loads((OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/report.json').read_text())
    return dict(prior_diagnosis=prior['diagnosis'],prior_ledger_audit=prior['ledger_audit'],conclusions=['Short recent cell windows confound estimation and calibration; date-common noise forbids stock count as independent N.','Frozen partial pooling uses neighboring cells only from training; independent block residuals calibrate uncertainty without fitting mean.','Five-date blocks address short date correlation but reduce effective sample. Missing cell dates reduce support further.','No holdout online fit or residual bias fit: safety latches independently, never narrows intervals.','No observable precursors to reversal in DGP; pre-maturation protection is information-limited.','Original manager accepts caller conditional_ev; it does not provide a class estimator. Prior causal ledger certifies timestamps, not calibration.'])
def main():
    start=time.monotonic();plan=json.loads((OUT/'preregister.json').read_text());ph=sha(OUT/'preregister.json')
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','unittest','discover','-s',str(OUT),'-p','test_*.py','-v'],capture_output=True,text=True)
    (OUT/'tests.log').write_text(result.stdout+result.stderr)
    if result.returncode:
        (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='STOP_TEST_FAILURE',full_portfolio_replays=0)));raise SystemExit(result.returncode)
    sources=list(b.a.SOURCES)+[b.PRIOR/'corrected_manager.py',b.PRIOR/'corrected_kernel.py',Path(b.__file__),OUT/'experiment.py',OUT/'test_iteration4.py',OUT/'preregister.json']
    sources+=list((OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination').glob('*.py'))
    sources+=[OUT.parent/'five_tier_discrimination/report.json',OUT.parent/'opportunity_evaluator_fix/report.json',OUT.parent.parent/'mts_v5_manager_repair_20261031/v5_working_copy/engines/counterfactual_learning/causal_ledger.py']
    sources += [Path(previous_model.__file__), OUT/'model.py', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/report.json', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/preregister.json', OUT.parent/'discrimination_iteration2/README.md']
    before={str(p):sha(p) for p in sources};diagnosis=diagnose();scores=[];metrics=[];attempts=0;opps=[];riskmax=0.;buys=0
    with gzip.open(OUT/'opportunities.jsonl.gz','wt') as of,gzip.open(OUT/'replays.jsonl.gz','wt') as rf:
        for seed in plan['seeds']:
            for scenario in plan['scenarios']:
                assert time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                days,coarse,f,p,truth=generate(seed,scenario)
                bundle,ledger,alarms=predict(days,f);new=bundle["iteration4"];previous,oldledger,oldalarms=previous_model.predict(days,f);ids=[list(range(24)) for _ in range(252)]
                for variant,features,cells,als in [('iteration4',ids,new,alarms),('iteration2_repaired',f,previous,oldalarms)]+[(v,ids,bundle[v],[]) for v in ('ungated','sensor_only','price_only')]:
                    score,rows=b.discrimination(days,features,p,truth,cells)
                    score.pop('cells',None)
                    scores.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,alarms=als,reversal_lag=next((z['index']-126 for z in als if z['index']>=126),None) if scenario=='reversal' else None,**score))
                    for z in rows:
                        z.update(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant);of.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                    for t in (*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'):
                        v=[z for z in rows if z['inferred_tier']==t]
                        opps.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,tier=t,n=len(v),coverage=len(v)/len(rows),precision=st.mean(z['true_ev_tier']==t for z in v) if v and t!='UNKNOWN' else None,realized_net=st.mean(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if v else None,abstained_positive_net_sum=sum(max(0,z['realized_net']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_signed_net_sum=sum(z['realized_net'] for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0,abstained_positive_ev_sum=sum(max(0,z['true_ev']) for z in v) if t=='UNKNOWN' else 0))
                with gzip.open(OUT/f'ledger_{seed}_{scenario}.jsonl.gz','wt') as lf:
                    for z in ledger:lf.write(json.dumps(z)+'\n')
                for variant,features,cells,policy in [('iteration4',ids,new,'tier10'),('iteration2_repaired',f,previous,'tier10'),('ungated',ids,bundle['ungated'],'tier10'),('scaled',ids,new,'tier15')]:
                    assert attempts<plan['max_full_replays'] and time.monotonic()-start<plan['max_seconds']
                    attempts+=1;(OUT/'attempts.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(attempts=attempts,seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant)))
                    kw=b.callbacks(features,p,cells,policy,days);evdays=days[126:252]
                    candidates=[m.Candidate(t,'GA7','synthetic',.02,s.GAPS[j]) for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS)]
                    result=m.run_slice(evdays,candidates,max_sessions=126,max_stocks=24,**s.COSTS,**kw)
                    metric,_=s.measure(result,evdays);metric['total_return']=metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL-1
                    metric['cagr']=(metric['final_nav']/s.INITIAL)**2-1
                    metric['fees']=sum(z[5] for z in result['ledger'])
                    metric['spread_slippage_impact_cost']=sum(z[3]*abs(z[4]-dict(evdays)[z[0]][z[1]].open) for z in result['ledger'])
                    assert all(z[2]>=-1e-8 for z in result['nav'])
                    metrics.append(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,**metric))
                    # Independent full post-BUY position risk, reconstructed from actual fills/NAV.
                    previous_shares={};bydate={d:[] for d,_ in evdays}
                    for fill in result['ledger']:bydate[fill[0]].append(fill)
                    for (d,bars),nav in zip(evdays,result['nav']):
                        fills=bydate[d];opening=nav[1]+sum(z[5]+z[3]*abs(z[4]-bars[z[1]].open) for z in fills)
                        for z in fills:
                            if z[2]=='BUY':
                                loss=(previous_shares.get(z[1],0)+z[3])*bars[z[1]].open/opening*(max(.02,s.GAPS[int(z[1][3:])])+s.ROUND_TRIP)
                                assert loss<=.02+1e-10;buys+=1;riskmax=max(riskmax,loss)
                        previous_shares=nav[3]
                    rf.write(json.dumps(dict(seed=seed,scenario=scenario,variant=variant,ledger=result['ledger'],nav=result['nav'],risk=result['risk_audit'],decisions=result['decisions']))+'\n')
                print(f'{seed} {scenario} {attempts}/{plan["max_full_replays"]} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s',flush=True)
    paired=[]
    for z in metrics:
        if z['variant']!='iteration4':continue
        for comparator in ('iteration2_repaired','ungated','scaled'):
            old=next(v for v in metrics if v['seed']==z['seed'] and v['scenario']==z['scenario'] and v['variant']==comparator)
            paired.append(dict(seed=z['seed'],scenario=z['scenario'],comparator=comparator,return_delta=z['total_return']-old['total_return'],mdd_delta=z['mdd']-old['mdd'],iteration3_return=z['total_return'],comparator_return=old['total_return']))
    assert all(sha(p)==h for p,h in before.items());assert sha(OUT/'preregister.json')==ph
    for score in scores:
        with (OUT/f'confusion_{score["seed"]}_{score["scenario"]}_{score["variant"]}.csv').open('w') as cf:
            w=csv.writer(cf);w.writerow(['true / predicted',*TIERS,'UNKNOWN'])
            for i,t in enumerate(TIERS):w.writerow([t,*score['confusion_ev'][i],score['unknown_by_ev_tier'][i]])
    writecsv('tier_metrics.csv',opps);writecsv('portfolio_runs.csv',metrics);writecsv('paired.csv',paired)
    report=dict(status='PASS' if all(z['status']=='PASS' for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration4' and z['scenario']!='null') and all(z['coverage']==0 for z in scores if z['variant']=='iteration4' and z['scenario']=='null') else 'FAIL',execution_status='COMPLETE',full_portfolio_replays=attempts,elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,preregister_sha256=ph,source_sha256=before,production_sources_unchanged=True,diagnosis=diagnosis,discrimination=scores,portfolio_runs=metrics,paired=paired,tier_metrics=opps,independent_risk_audit=dict(actual_buys=buys,max_candidate_modeled_loss=riskmax,passed=True),limitations=plan['limitations'])
    (OUT/'report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(report['status'])

if __name__=="__main__":main()
```

### Method source model.py
```python
"""Experiment-local frozen models and separate causal reliability policy."""
from reference import *

def predictors(days, sensors):
    out=[]
    for i in range(252):
        rows=[]
        for j,t in enumerate(s.TICKERS):
            returns=[];sector=[];market=[]
            for h in range(max(1,i-19),i+1):
                def ret(q):
                    try:return days[h][1][q].close/days[h-1][1][q].close-1
                    except (KeyError,ZeroDivisionError):return 0.
                returns.append(ret(t));sector.append(st.mean(ret(q) for q in s.TICKERS[(j//6)*6:(j//6+1)*6]));market.append(st.mean(ret(q) for q in s.TICKERS))
            vol=max(.005,st.pstdev(returns)) if returns else .005
            trend=st.mean(returns) if returns else 0.
            relative=trend-(st.mean(sector) if sector else 0.)
            residual=trend-(st.mean(market) if market else 0.)
            price=[relative/.01,residual/.01,trend/vol,vol/.01]
            rows.append([1.]+[float(sensors[i][j]==c) for c in range(4)]+price)
        out.append(rows)
    return out

def solve(rows, cols):
    n=len(cols);a=[[float(x==y) for y in range(n)] for x in range(n)];v=[0.]*n
    for x,y in rows:
        z=[x[c] for c in cols]
        for h in range(n):
            v[h]+=z[h]*y
            for q in range(n):a[h][q]+=z[h]*z[q]
    for h in range(n):
        pivot=a[h][h]
        for q in range(h,n):a[h][q]/=pivot
        v[h]/=pivot
        for z in range(n):
            if z==h:continue
            f=a[z][h]
            for q in range(h,n):a[z][q]-=f*a[h][q]
            v[z]-=f*v[h]
    return v

def forecast(beta,x,cols):return sum(b*x[c] for b,c in zip(beta,cols))

def fit(days,x,cols):
    rows=[(x[i][j],observed(days,i,j)) for i in range(20,78) for j in range(24)]
    beta=solve(rows,cols)
    # Independent date-cluster residual bias; five-date block uncertainty.
    residual=[st.mean(observed(days,i,j)-forecast(beta,x[i][j],cols) for j in range(24)) for i in range(80,122)]
    blocks=[st.mean(residual[i:i+5]) for i in range(0,40,5)]
    bias=abs(st.mean(blocks));se=st.stdev(blocks)/len(blocks)**.5
    # Include training date residual SE, without treating stocks as dates.
    tr=[st.mean(observed(days,i,j)-forecast(beta,x[i][j],cols) for j in range(24)) for i in range(20,78)]
    radius=bias+2.58*math.hypot(se,st.stdev(tr)/len(tr)**.5)
    return dict(beta=beta,cols=cols,radius=radius,bias=bias)

def gate(disagreement,recent,drift,latched=False):
    reason='DETERIORATION' if recent is not None and abs(recent)>.004 else 'FEATURE_DRIFT' if drift>4 else 'FORECAST_DISAGREEMENT' if disagreement>.004 else 'RELIABLE'
    return not latched and reason=='RELIABLE',reason,latched or reason in ('DETERIORATION','FEATURE_DRIFT')

def predict(days,sensors):
    x=predictors(days,sensors);models=None;states={k:[] for k in ('iteration4','ungated','sensor_only','price_only')};ledger=[];alarms=[];latched=False
    center=[st.mean(x[i][j][c] for i in range(20,78) for j in range(24)) for c in range(9)]
    scale=[max(.1,st.pstdev(x[i][j][c] for i in range(20,78) for j in range(24))) for c in range(9)]
    for now in range(252):
        for row in ledger[max(0,(now-3)*24):max(0,(now-2)*24)]:
            if row['index']!=now-3:continue
            try:row.update(realized_net=observed(days,row['index'],row['j']),available_index=now,missingness='OBSERVED')
            except (KeyError,IndexError):row['missingness']='MISSING'
        if now==126:models={name:fit(days,x,cols) for name,cols in [('full',list(range(9))),('sensor',list(range(5))),('price',[0,5,6,7,8])]}
        recentrows=[z for z in ledger if now-8<=z['index']<now-2 and z['index']>=126 and z['realized_net'] is not None]
        datebias={}
        for z in recentrows:datebias.setdefault(z['index'],[]).append(z['realized_net']-z['inferred_net'])
        recent=st.mean(st.mean(v) for v in datebias.values()) if len(datebias)>=3 else None
        current={k:[] for k in states}
        for j in range(24):
            preds={k:forecast(v['beta'],x[now][j],v['cols']) for k,v in models.items()} if models else dict(full=0.,sensor=0.,price=0.)
            drift=max(abs(x[now][j][c]-center[c])/scale[c] for c in range(5,9)) if now>=126 else 0.
            ok,reason,latch=gate(max(preds.values())-min(preds.values()),recent,drift,latched)
            if latch and not latched:alarms.append(dict(index=now,reason=reason,last_signal=now-3));latched=True
            for variant,key in [('iteration4','full'),('ungated','full'),('sensor_only','sensor'),('price_only','price')]:
                mu=preds[key];radius=models[key]['radius'] if models else 1.
                label=infer(mu,radius,58 if models else 0,42 if models else 0)
                if variant=='iteration4' and not ok:label='UNKNOWN'
                current[variant].append(dict(mean=mu,radius=radius,label=label,reason=reason if variant=='iteration4' else 'UNGATED',latest_outcome_signal=now-3,reliability=float(ok),ntrain=58 if models else 0,ncal=42 if models else 0))
            if now<250:ledger.append(dict(index=now,j=j,execution_index=now+1,maturity_index=now+2,available_index=None,realized_net=None,missingness='PENDING',inferred_net=preds['full'],features=x[now][j],feature_latest_index=now,feature_source='close<=decision; fixed six-symbol synthetic sector proxy',volume=None,revisions=None,inferred_tier=current['iteration4'][-1]['label'],gate_reason=reason))
        for key in states:states[key].append(current[key])
    return states,ledger,alarms
```
